# Overview of UniSat 2025 Work Priorities | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities.md)
.

### Work carried-over from 2024[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#work-carried-over-from-2024)

#### **CAT721**[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#cat721)

Let’s start with some ongoing work.

Support for CAT721 on Fractal is expected to be delivered in two phases: wallet and browser support will be delivered by January 10th, and CAT721 market support will be delivered around January 20th, with corresponding community events.

🟠 Updates from Feb. 2025: The CAT721 marketplace development is complete and awaiting launch. Stay tuned.

#### **InSwap**[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#inswap)

There will be three updates in a row:

a. Simplifying user operations through embedding the bridge in the swap.

b. Implementing support for Runes and CAT20.

c. Introducing support for the stablecoin SUSD from Shell Finance.

These changes will be delivered over different iterations throughout January.

🟠 Updates:

✅ Integrated bridge + deposit in one step, dramatically reducing deposit and withdrawal waiting times.

✅ InSwap integrated SUSD•SUSD•SUSD (Runes) — a stablecoin developed by Shell Finance based on the Runes Protocol.

➕ Additional updates and improvements:

*   New domain and multilingual support: [https://inswap.cc](https://inswap.cc/)
     now available in multiple languages including Traditional Chinese.
    

➕ Beyond Feature/UI upgrades:

*   Added LP Burn feature
    
*   Introduced One-click sign for swaps
    
*   Enabled Custom fee rates for wrap operations
    
*   Added Bulk Send for brc-20 transfers to multiple addresses
    
*   Enhanced charts and pool information for a smoother experience
    

➕ Events

*   Regular LPfest epochs: Epochs 3, 3.1, 4, and 4.1 successfully completed
    
*   InSwap Liquidity Boost & LP Match events
    
*   InSwap trading events with community pairs
    

* * *

### Product Deliverables for 2025[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#product-deliverables-for-2025)

#### **brc-20 Feature Extensions and Open-Source Indexing Service**[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#brc-20-feature-extensions-and-open-source-indexing-service)

Considering the inconsistencies that have existed with brc-20 on the Bitcoin mainnet, we made a few adjustments to significantly simplify and reduce potential rule complexities for brc-20 on Fractal. These include supporting 6-12 character ticker names, lifting the character constraints for fair-mint and self-issuance, using a simplified standard character set, and so on. For more details, see [here](https://docs.fractalbitcoin.io/doc/brc-20-on-fractal)
.

In terms of expanding brc-20 features, we will attempt further improvements in 2025 on Fractal, including:

*   **One-step transfer:** Simplifying the current mint and transfer into a single step.
    
*   **One-to-many transfer:** Enabling a single transfer to multiple users.
    
*   **Airdrop functionality:** Supporting airdrop transactions with up to 2000 recipients in a single transaction.
    
*   **Phased minting:** Enabling fair minting within a specified block range.
    

We believe that enhancing and expanding these features will make brc-20 a more featured protocol.

Regarding implementation, UniSat will focus more on refining and improving its open-source indexing service. While the previously released open-source index service works, it hasn’t been actively maintained. Our improvement objectives include:

*   Simplifying the system architecture of the open-source index, focusing solely on providing brc-20 index services, without the need to maintain flexible architecture supporting multiple protocols.
    
*   Fully integrating UniSat’s high-performance proprietary brc-20 indexing code into the open-source index service, delivering significant advantages over the official Ordinals index service. Specifically, our high-performance indexing service can process hundreds of blocks per second, with full on-chain data processing taking only a few hours, while the Ordinals index processes one block in a few seconds and requires at least two days to process complete on-chain data.
    
*   In the near future, we aim to transition UniSat’s entire product line to use this open-source index implementation and maintain it actively for the developer community.
    

Long-term, we hope to optimize the index service implementation, introduce new features, and create applications to inject new vitality into brc-20, driving the continued development for the protocol.

🟠 Updates:

✅ brc-20 Single-Step Transfer already live on Fractal Mainnet

✅ Our UTXO Management Tool was officially launched on Fractal & Bitcoin, along with continuous feature enhancements including:

*   Merge, One-click merge, Unlock/Lock, Batch send to multiple addresses, and Split functions.
    
*   These updates provide better asset organization and meet developer needs such as large-scale airdrops.
    

#### UniSat Wallet 2.0[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#unisat-wallet-2.0)

We will deliver version 2.0 of UniSat Wallet in Q2. The most critical change will be the security model upgrade. Users will be able to choose from different security models and, when needed, use cross-device interactions for higher security. This version will also support more types of hardware wallets, such as Ledger and Trezor, and gradually support more protocols on Bitcoin.

We’ve been taking note of community feedback, highly requested features such as easier inscription management (categorization and search), handling obsoleted inscriptions or large amount of scattered inscriptions, and managing fragmented UTXOs will be delivered across multiple product updates throughout 2025.

🟠 Updates:

✅ Wallet Enhancements

*   Added multilingual support: French, Spanish, Russian, Japanese, and Traditional Chinese.
    
*   Added support for Alkanes & brc2.0 assets
    
*   Introduced Address Book, Feedback Channel, and improved password UI for smoother user experience.
    

✅ Launched UTXO Management Tool on UniSat wallet, featuring:

*   Clear categorization by asset type for easier and more efficient asset management.
    
*   Detailed breakdown of assets within each UTXO.
    
*   Gradually introduced Merge, One-click merge, Unlock/Lock, Batch send, and Split functions.
    
*   The tool now supports both Fractal and Bitcoin networks, allowing seamless network switching.
    

#### UniSat Explorer 2.0[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#unisat-explorer-2.0)

In Q4 of 2024, with the launch of Fractal, we launched a comprehensive block explorer for the Fractal network- UniSat Explorer. This explorer is compatible with different protocols and clearly show asset transfers across protocols like Ordinals, brc-20, Runes, and cat20.

In the new year, we will further enhance UniSat Explorer’s capabilities, expanding to Bitcoin mainnet and ensuring product consistency across different networks. For example, users will be able to conveniently query the asset distribution of a Bitcoin address across different networks with one click.

🟠 Updates:

➕ Apr 2025: Launched the new UniScan domain [https://uniscan.cc](https://uniscan.cc/)

✅ Expanded Fractal Explorer’s capabilities to the Bitcoin mainnet — clearly displaying cross-protocol transfers for Ordinals, brc-20, Runes, and cat20 assets.

✅ Continued to enhance UniScan with expanded multi-protocol compatibility, now supporting Alkanes, brc2.0, and more.

➕ Beyond Feature/UI upgrades:

*   Heat Map launched — making it easier to track trending transactions on Fractal and Bitcoin.
    
*   Enhanced Wallet Address Display for better clarity and visibility.
    
*   Sort your assets by $ value for a clearer portfolio overview.
    
*   InSwap assets and transaction details are now visible directly in your wallet.
    

#### UniSat Marketplace 2.0[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#unisat-marketplace-2.0)

This year, we will fully upgrade the PSBT-based P2P transaction model used by our Marketplace. We are gearing up for a major product and brand upgrade of the Marketplace in Q3. Stay tuned for community events around the release.

* * *

### Collaboration and Ecosystem Development Plans for 2025[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#collaboration-and-ecosystem-development-plans-for-2025)

#### Bitcoin/Fractal Interoperability[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#bitcoin-fractal-interoperability)

In 2025, our main focus for Fractal Bitcoin will be to enhance interoperability between Bitcoin and Fractal, simplifying asset flow between Bitcoin’s mainnet and Fractal. This will allow Fractal to play a more substantial role in supporting Bitcoin as a system and lay better groundwork for using Fractal to expand Bitcoin. Over the long term, we aim to create a more scalable and flexible system for Bitcoin/Fractal.

The launch of DeTrading, a trustless cross-chain platform for atomic swaps that supports Fractal and Bitcoin networks, marks a major milestone in Bitcoin interoperability. This groundbreaking application of atomic swaps is the first successful attempt to bring a truly trustless cross-chain experience for users.

DeTrading’s solution will play significant role in simplifying and promoting interoperability between Fractal Bitcoin and Bitcoin mainnet. The atomic swap protocol can also support multiple asset types including brc-20, runes and even CAt20, and be expanded to other blockchains like Ethereum and Solana, which makes it a highly universal and adaptable approach. This universal approach paves way for fast transaction channels for different types of assets across various networks. UniSat will support the DeTrading team in refining and improving their product and may also explore structural improvements to existing bridge products.

✅ Updates: DeTrading has officially launched on the UniSat Dapps portal, enabling seamless FB ⇄ BTC swaps directly on-chain.

#### Constructing a Hyper Indexer[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#constructing-a-hyper-indexer)

In 2025, SatWorld will start to create a vibrant virtual world, allowing people to create (avatars), explore (vast unknown worlds), harvest (treasures and resources), build (homes and habitats), and organize various forms of interaction. The virtual world will also have autonomous agents that interact with user avatars, offering more possibilities and variety.

UniSat will collaborate closely with SatWorld, using the Bitcoin/Fractal network infrastructure to help bring this vision into reality. We will improve and optimize the existing indexer, provide a more user-friendly interface that allows virtual world creators to quickly access and use images, sounds, texts, artworks, and concepts existing in millions of inscriptions. By turning on-chain inscription data into a shared library and development repository, UniSat’s indexing service will become a powerful hyper-indexer for managing and retrieving on-chain resources.

#### DID System Based on Unified Addresses[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#did-system-based-on-unified-addresses)

One unique feature of Fractal Bitcoin is its shared address format and transaction structure with Bitcoin, this not only allow Bitcoin users to enjoy Fractal’s expanded block space and utility with ease, but also allows us to create build a global identity and reputation system in the Bitcoin system. You may have noticed that UniSat has started creating different Titles that users can claim for unlocking various achievements. With this system, Users will be able to form and leverage a universal identity and reputation based on their activity and achievements across networks.

In the new year, we will gradually begin to build out this system, starting with the following:

*   UniSat titles
    
*   Domains linked to your Bitcoin/Fractal addresses.
    
*   Interactions with different projects in the Bitcoin/Fractal ecosystem.
    
*   Your on-chain voting history in Fractal Vote.
    
*   Your avatars and gear in SatWorld.
    
*   Changes in your Fractal Bitcoin and other asset holdings.
    

This list will expand as the system is developed. You will gradually see the system rolled out on your UniSat personal asset page. We will accumulate a DID system based on on-chain reputation, allowing you to link addresses used in different periods as part of your identity.

🟠 Updates:

✅ The UniSat Titles system was officially launched — each title marks a moment in time when you’ve helped make Bitcoin grow and thrive.

✅ We also introduced Drops — our way to show our love for Art on Bitcoin and to say thank you to the community by awarding benefits based on Proof of Contribution.

*   Drops creatively uses UniSat Titles as eligibility to participate so creators can get maximum exposure to UniSat Title.
    
*   Throughout 2025, the Titles system continues to grow, integrating with more use cases and evolving into an increasingly comprehensive reputation system.
    

✅ Through active interactions with projects across Fractal ecosystem, we have launched multiple events — including LPfest, LPmatch, InSwap Liquidity Boost, and various InSwap trading campaigns.

#### Support for Stablecoins, Yields, AI Agents, and More[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#support-for-stablecoins-yields-ai-agents-and-more)

We have already done some preliminary work on stablecoins. Some time in 2025, we plan to begin to provide strong support for stablecoins in our products. For Fractal, stablecoins with low fees will become a critical part of transactions, thereby driving the development of more use cases.

In December 2024, UniSat Wallet integrated Babylon's service. We will also engage with more projects that generate yield from holding assets and actively explore native yield models on Fractal Bitcoin.

We have observed that active AI agents are beginning to support Bitcoin, and we will contribute code to support Ordinals, Runes, and Fractal Bitcoin, ensuring that these agents can operate inscriptions and other assets without obstacles.

✅ Updates: In Sep 2025, We officially launched FB Farming, the first native, fully on-chain, non-custodial yield solution for the Fractal Bitcoin community. FB Farming is designed to reward long-term FB holders and promote sustainable ecosystem growth.

* * *

### Conclusion[](https://docs.unisat.io/welcome/unisat-work-priorities/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#conclusion)

This document shares a brief overview of our development plans for 2025. As our work progresses, we will update this plan periodically. In the coming year, we will focus on integrating development and operational resources more effectively, serving Bitcoin users more holistically, and fostering a more open industry atmosphere. We aim to collaborate more actively with different teams and communities, explore innovative ideas, contribute more open-source code, and organize more community activities, creating greater value and opportunities for users and developers.

_(This document will be updated as work progresses, Last Update: 2025-01-05)_

This section outlines what we’re actively building, improving, or exploring. It reflects our evolving priorities as we grow the UniSat ecosystem, while staying responsive to community feedback and protocol-level changes.

What you'll find here:\\

🛠️ Feature and product development priorities

📊 Infrastructure, ecosystem, and scalability work

We believe in building in the open — this page is part of that commitment.

Note: Items listed here are not guaranteed release targets or deadlines. Priorities may shift based on technical discoveries, user demand, or ecosystem needs.

For the latest product updates or changelogs, please refer to our Release Notes or Dev Updates on X.

# Production Roadmap in Q4 2024

As we enter the final quarter of 2024, our focus remains on delivering and optimizing our products to provide top-tier infrastructure support for both Bitcoin and Fractal. We are excited to share our upcoming roadmap to encourage proactive and meaningful engagement with the community as we continue our journey together.

\*(please note this is a draft version that will be revised periodically as we progress)\*

## Roadmap in Q4 2024

### October 2024

\* \*\*Runes activation on Fractal\*\*

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Implement Runes on Fractal (lowercase)&#x20;
  \* Post-activation: Enable SimpleBridge to bridge Bitcoin mainnet Runes onto Fractal,  so that they can interact with Runes on Fractal directly

\* \*\*CAT20 identifying and transferring\*\*&#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* UniSat Wallet: Enable CAT20 recognition, display and transfers
  \* UniSat Explorer: Identify CAT20 assets and transactions &#x20;

\* \*\*CAT Market (trustless CAT20 trading)\*\*&#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Implement a trustless, permissionless Bitcoin script-based on-chain CAT20 trading platform&#x20;
  \* Support for both buy orders and sell orders (more flexible than PSBT), but partial order fulfillment is not supported yet due to script limitations (without OP\\\_MUL)

### November 2024

\* \*\*Fractal on-chain voting\*\*&#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Design and implement OP\\\_CAT-based locking and voting model
  \* Apply transparent proposal submission, discussion and adoption for Fractal&#x20;

\* \*\*InSwap major update\*\*&#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* UniSat Points and FB Rewards for specific Liquidity Providers
  \* Simplified operating procedures for InSwap
  \* Opening up to all tickers on Fractal.

\* \*\*brc-20, Runes & CAT20 optimization\*\*

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Enhance UniSat indexing infrastructure to achieve faster block parsing with all supporting protocols (target response time: 15-30s)
  \* Optimize the responsiveness (balance updating and UTXO retrieving) of UniSat Wallet (target response time: 15-30s)

### December 2024

\* \*\*zkAtomicSwap - effective atomic swapping\*\*&#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Implement atomic swap between different chains & different protocols
  \* Trustless trading mechanism between Fractal and other ecosystem

\* \*\*CAT Protocol advanced support\*\*&#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Advanced support for cat721 and cat-dApps across all UniSat products
  \* Further consolidating product-side implementation of the CAT protocol

\* \*\*InSwap major update\*\* &#x20;

  \* (Engineering complexity: ⭐️⭐️⭐️; Technical innovation: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️; Scheduling certainty: ⭐️⭐️⭐️⭐️)
  \* Perform the upgrade of the BRC-20 swap module on Bitcoin mainnet.
  \* Build unified and consistent accessing model for both Bitcoin and Fractal for InSwap

UniSat Team

# Overview of UniSat 2025 Work Priorities

Note: This plan summary includes content related to our ongoing work and plan for 2025, with a focus on development work.

### Work carried-over from 2024

#### \*\*CAT721\*\*

Let’s start with some ongoing work.

Support for CAT721 on Fractal is expected to be delivered in two phases: wallet and browser support will be delivered by January 10th, and CAT721 market support will be delivered around January 20th, with corresponding community events.&#x20;

#### \*\*InSwap\*\*

There will be three updates in a row:

a. Simplifying user operations through embedding the bridge in the swap.&#x20;

b. Implementing support for Runes and CAT20.&#x20;

c. Introducing support for the stablecoin SUSD from Shell Finance.

These changes will be delivered over different iterations throughout January.

&#x20;🟠 Updates:&#x20;

✅ Integrated bridge + deposit in one step, dramatically reducing deposit and withdrawal waiting times.&#x20;

✅ InSwap integrated SUSD•SUSD•SUSD (Runes) — a stablecoin developed by Shell Finance based on the Runes Protocol.

➕ Additional updates and improvements:

➕ Beyond Feature/UI upgrades:

\* Introduced One-click sign for swaps
\* Enabled Custom fee rates for wrap operations
\* Added Bulk Send for brc-20 transfers to multiple addresses
\* Enhanced charts and pool information for a smoother experience

➕ Events

\* Regular LPfest epochs: Epochs 3, 3.1, 4, and 4.1 successfully completed
\* InSwap Liquidity Boost & LP Match events
\* InSwap trading events with community pairs

\*\*\*

### Product Deliverables for 2025

#### \*\*brc-20 Feature Extensions and Open-Source Indexing Service\*\*

Considering the inconsistencies that have existed with brc-20 on the Bitcoin mainnet, we made a few adjustments to significantly simplify and reduce potential rule complexities for brc-20 on Fractal. These include supporting 6-12 character ticker names, lifting the character constraints for fair-mint and self-issuance, using a simplified standard character set, and so on. For more details, see \[here\](https://docs.fractalbitcoin.io/doc/brc-20-on-fractal).

In terms of expanding brc-20 features, we will attempt further improvements in 2025 on Fractal, including:

\* \*\*One-step transfer:\*\* Simplifying the current mint and transfer into a single step.
\* \*\*One-to-many transfer:\*\* Enabling a single transfer to multiple users.
\* \*\*Airdrop functionality:\*\* Supporting airdrop transactions with up to 2000 recipients in a single transaction.
\* \*\*Phased minting:\*\* Enabling fair minting within a specified block range.

We believe that enhancing and expanding these features will make brc-20 a more featured protocol.

Regarding implementation, UniSat will focus more on refining and improving its open-source indexing service. While the previously released open-source index service works, it hasn’t been actively maintained. Our improvement objectives include:

\* Simplifying the system architecture of the open-source index, focusing solely on providing brc-20 index services, without the need to maintain flexible architecture supporting multiple protocols.
\* Fully integrating UniSat’s high-performance proprietary brc-20 indexing code into the open-source index service, delivering significant advantages over the official Ordinals index service. Specifically, our high-performance indexing service can process hundreds of blocks per second, with full on-chain data processing taking only a few hours, while the Ordinals index processes one block in a few seconds and requires at least two days to process complete on-chain data.
\* In the near future, we aim to transition UniSat’s entire product line to use this open-source index implementation and maintain it actively for the developer community.

Long-term, we hope to optimize the index service implementation, introduce new features, and create applications to inject new vitality into brc-20, driving the continued development for the protocol.

&#x20;🟠 Updates:&#x20;

✅ brc-20 Single-Step Transfer already live on Fractal Mainnet

✅ Our UTXO Management Tool was officially launched on Fractal & Bitcoin, along with continuous feature enhancements including:

\* Merge, One-click merge, Unlock/Lock, Batch send to multiple addresses, and Split functions.
\* These updates provide better asset organization and meet developer needs such as large-scale airdrops.

#### UniSat Wallet 2.0

We will deliver version 2.0 of UniSat Wallet in Q2. The most critical change will be the security model upgrade. Users will be able to choose from different security models and, when needed, use cross-device interactions for higher security. This version will also support more types of hardware wallets, such as Ledger and Trezor, and gradually support more protocols on Bitcoin.

We’ve been taking note of community feedback, highly requested features such as easier inscription management (categorization and search), handling obsoleted inscriptions or large amount of scattered inscriptions, and managing fragmented UTXOs will be delivered across multiple product updates throughout 2025.

🟠 Updates:

✅ Wallet Enhancements

\* Added multilingual support: French, Spanish, Russian, Japanese, and Traditional Chinese.
\* Added support for Alkanes & brc2.0 assets
\* Introduced Address Book, Feedback Channel, and improved password UI for smoother user experience.

✅ Launched UTXO Management Tool on UniSat wallet, featuring:

\* Clear categorization by asset type for easier and more efficient asset management.
\* Detailed breakdown of assets within each UTXO.
\* Gradually introduced Merge, One-click merge, Unlock/Lock, Batch send, and Split functions.
\* The tool now supports both Fractal and Bitcoin networks, allowing seamless network switching.

#### UniSat Explorer 2.0

In Q4 of 2024, with the launch of Fractal, we launched a comprehensive block explorer for the Fractal network- UniSat Explorer. This explorer is compatible with different protocols and clearly show asset transfers across protocols like Ordinals, brc-20, Runes, and cat20.

In the new year, we will further enhance UniSat Explorer’s capabilities, expanding to Bitcoin mainnet and ensuring product consistency across different networks. For example, users will be able to conveniently query the asset distribution of a Bitcoin address across different networks with one click.

🟠 Updates:

➕ Apr 2025: Launched the new UniScan domain \[https://uniscan.cc\](https://uniscan.cc/)

✅ Expanded Fractal Explorer’s capabilities to the Bitcoin mainnet — clearly displaying cross-protocol transfers for Ordinals, brc-20, Runes, and cat20 assets.

✅ Continued to enhance UniScan with expanded multi-protocol compatibility, now supporting Alkanes, brc2.0, and more.

➕ Beyond Feature/UI upgrades:

\* Heat Map launched — making it easier to track trending transactions on Fractal and Bitcoin.
\* Enhanced Wallet Address Display for better clarity and visibility.
\* Sort your assets by $ value for a clearer portfolio overview.
\* InSwap assets and transaction details are now visible directly in your wallet.

#### UniSat Marketplace 2.0

This year, we will fully upgrade the PSBT-based P2P transaction model used by our Marketplace. We are gearing up for a major product and brand upgrade of the Marketplace in Q3. Stay tuned for community events around the release.

\*\*\*

### Collaboration and Ecosystem Development Plans for 2025

#### Bitcoin/Fractal Interoperability

In 2025, our main focus for Fractal Bitcoin will be to enhance interoperability between Bitcoin and Fractal, simplifying asset flow between Bitcoin’s mainnet and Fractal. This will allow Fractal to play a more substantial role in supporting Bitcoin as a system and lay better groundwork for using Fractal to expand Bitcoin. Over the long term, we aim to create a more scalable and flexible system for Bitcoin/Fractal.

The launch of DeTrading, a trustless cross-chain platform for atomic swaps that supports Fractal and Bitcoin networks, marks a major milestone in Bitcoin interoperability. This groundbreaking application of atomic swaps is the first successful attempt to bring a truly trustless cross-chain experience for users.

DeTrading’s solution will play significant role in simplifying and promoting interoperability between Fractal Bitcoin and Bitcoin mainnet. The atomic swap protocol can also support multiple asset types including brc-20, runes and even CAt20, and be expanded to other blockchains like Ethereum and Solana, which makes it a highly universal and adaptable approach. This universal approach paves way for fast transaction channels for different types of assets across various networks. UniSat will support the DeTrading team in refining and improving their product and may also explore structural improvements to existing bridge products.

✅ Updates: DeTrading has officially launched on the UniSat Dapps portal, enabling seamless FB ⇄ BTC swaps directly on-chain.

#### Constructing a Hyper Indexer

In 2025, SatWorld will start to create a vibrant virtual world, allowing people to create (avatars), explore (vast unknown worlds), harvest (treasures and resources), build (homes and habitats), and organize various forms of interaction. The virtual world will also have autonomous agents that interact with user avatars, offering more possibilities and variety.

UniSat will collaborate closely with SatWorld, using the Bitcoin/Fractal network infrastructure to help bring this vision into reality. We will improve and optimize the existing indexer, provide a more user-friendly interface that allows virtual world creators to quickly access and use images, sounds, texts, artworks, and concepts existing in millions of inscriptions. By turning on-chain inscription data into a shared library and development repository, UniSat’s indexing service will become a powerful hyper-indexer for managing and retrieving on-chain resources.

#### DID System Based on Unified Addresses

One unique feature of Fractal Bitcoin is its shared address format and transaction structure with Bitcoin, this not only allow Bitcoin users to enjoy Fractal’s expanded block space and utility with ease, but also allows us to create build a global identity and reputation system in the Bitcoin system. You may have noticed that UniSat has started creating different Titles that users can claim for unlocking various achievements. With this system, Users will be able to form and leverage a universal identity and reputation based on their activity and achievements across networks.

In the new year, we will gradually begin to build out this system, starting with the following:

\* UniSat titles
\* Domains linked to your Bitcoin/Fractal addresses.
\* Interactions with different projects in the Bitcoin/Fractal ecosystem.
\* Your on-chain voting history in Fractal Vote.
\* Your avatars and gear in SatWorld.
\* Changes in your Fractal Bitcoin and other asset holdings.

This list will expand as the system is developed. You will gradually see the system rolled out on your UniSat personal asset page. We will accumulate a DID system based on on-chain reputation, allowing you to link addresses used in different periods as part of your identity.

🟠 Updates:&#x20;

✅ The UniSat Titles system was officially launched — each title marks a moment in time when you’ve helped make Bitcoin grow and thrive. &#x20;

✅ We also introduced Drops — our way to show our love for Art on Bitcoin and to say thank you to the community by awarding benefits based on Proof of Contribution.

\* Drops creatively uses UniSat Titles as eligibility to participate so creators can get maximum exposure to UniSat Title.&#x20;
\* Throughout 2025, the Titles system continues to grow, integrating with more use cases and evolving into an increasingly comprehensive reputation system.

✅ Through active interactions with projects across Fractal ecosystem, we have launched multiple events — including LPfest, LPmatch, InSwap Liquidity Boost, and various InSwap trading campaigns.<br>

#### Support for Stablecoins, Yields, AI Agents, and More

We have already done some preliminary work on stablecoins. Some time in 2025, we plan to begin to provide strong support for stablecoins in our products. For Fractal, stablecoins with low fees will become a critical part of transactions, thereby driving the development of more use cases.

In December 2024, UniSat Wallet integrated Babylon's service. We will also engage with more projects that generate yield from holding assets and actively explore native yield models on Fractal Bitcoin.

We have observed that active AI agents are beginning to support Bitcoin, and we will contribute code to support Ordinals, Runes, and Fractal Bitcoin, ensuring that these agents can operate inscriptions and other assets without obstacles.

✅ Updates:\\
In Sep 2025, We officially launched FB Farming, the first native, fully on-chain, non-custodial yield solution for the Fractal Bitcoin community. FB Farming is designed to reward long-term FB holders and promote sustainable ecosystem growth.

\*\*\*

### Conclusion

This document shares a brief overview of our development plans for 2025. As our work progresses, we will update this plan periodically. In the coming year, we will focus on integrating development and operational resources more effectively, serving Bitcoin users more holistically, and fostering a more open industry atmosphere. We aim to collaborate more actively with different teams and communities, explore innovative ideas, contribute more open-source code, and organize more community activities, creating greater value and opportunities for users and developers.

\*(This document will be updated as work progresses, Last Update: 2025-01-05)\*

# UniSat Wallet | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/products/unisat-wallet.md)
.

### **UniSat Wallet — Home for Your Bitcoin Assets**[](https://docs.unisat.io/products/unisat-wallet#unisat-wallet-home-for-your-bitcoin-assets)

_Easy and safe to trade, inscribe, and manage assets with UniSat_

UniSat wallet is the first fully open-source, non-custodial, and decentralized wallet built for Bitcoin’s innovative standards. it provides users with a decentralized, self-custody experience alongside a suite of integrated tools, including:

*   UniSat Marketplace
    
*   UniSat Inscribe
    
*   InSwap
    
*   UTXO Management Tool
    
*   DApp Center
    

Together, these services deliver a one-stop, seamless on-chain experience for Bitcoin and Fractal Bitcoin.

#### **Key Advantages of UniSat Wallet:**[](https://docs.unisat.io/products/unisat-wallet#key-advantages-of-unisat-wallet)

*   **Decentralized**/**Non-Custodial** **Control:** Your private keys, passwords, and seed phrases are never stored by UniSat. Users retain full control of their keys with no personal data collected.
    
*   **100% Open Source:** All code is available on GitHub, ensuring transparency, security, and community-driven development.
    
*   **Encryption & Security:** Private keys are encrypted locally with your password, providing robust protection for your assets.
    
*   **Privacy First:** UniSat does not track personal data. You can import private keys as "imported" accounts for flexible wallet management.
    
*   **All-in-One Bitcoin & Fractal:** UniSat Wallet supports Native SegWit, Taproot, Nested SegWit, and Legacy address types. You can manage BTC, Ordinals, Runes, and Alkanes in one place, with multi-seed import and batch address derivation. Seamlessly access UniSat Marketplace, Inscribe, InSwap, UniScan and other services, and switch between Bitcoin and Fractal with a single click.
    
*   **Multi-Platform Support:** Available on iOS, Android, and web browsers for convenient, cross-device access.
    

Being a non-custodial wallet means you will be in full control of managing and safeguarding your assets, including your seed phrase, private keys, and wallet password. You are the only one with access, and thus fully accountable for their security. You will need:

*   Back up your recovery phrase or private keys securely. Losing them means losing your assets permanently.
    
*   Set a strong wallet password and keep it safe. UniSat cannot help recover forgotten passwords.
    
*   Only download UniSat Wallet from the [official website](https://unisat.io/download)
     to avoid fraud or phishing.
    
*   Be cautious when clicking links or downloading software from any source.
    

Stay safe and enjoy your journey with UniSat Wallet!

#### Disclaimer:[](https://docs.unisat.io/products/unisat-wallet#disclaimer)

*   Your avatars and gear in SatWorld.
    
*   Changes in your Fractal Bitcoin and other asset holdings.
    

This list will expand as the system is developed. You will gradually see the system rolled out on your UniSat personal asset page. We will accumulate a DID system based on on-chain reputation, allowing you to link addresses used in different periods as part of your identity.

🟠 Updates:

✅ The UniSat Titles system was officially launched — each title marks a moment in time when you’ve helped make Bitcoin grow and thrive.

✅ We also introduced Drops — our way to show our love for Art on Bitcoin and to say thank you to the community by awarding benefits based on Proof of Contribution.

*   Drops creatively uses UniSat Titles as eligibility to participate so creators can get maximum exposure to UniSat Title.
    
*   Throughout 2025, the Titles system continues to grow, integrating with more use cases and evolving into an increasingly comprehensive reputation system.
    

✅ Through active interactions with projects across Fractal ecosystem, we have launched multiple events — including LPfest, LPmatch, InSwap Liquidity Boost, and various InSwap trading campaigns.

#### Support for Stablecoins, Yields, AI Agents, and More[](https://docs.unisat.io/home/production-roadmap/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#support-for-stablecoins-yields-ai-agents-and-more)

We have already done some preliminary work on stablecoins. Some time in 2025, we plan to begin to provide strong support for stablecoins in our products. For Fractal, stablecoins with low fees will become a critical part of transactions, thereby driving the development of more use cases.

In December 2024, UniSat Wallet integrated Babylon's service. We will also engage with more projects that generate yield from holding assets and actively explore native yield models on Fractal Bitcoin.

We have observed that active AI agents are beginning to support Bitcoin, and we will contribute code to support Ordinals, Runes, and Fractal Bitcoin, ensuring that these agents can operate inscriptions and other assets without obstacles.

✅ Updates: In Sep 2025, We officially launched FB Farming, the first native, fully on-chain, non-custodial yield solution for the Fractal Bitcoin community. FB Farming is designed to reward long-term FB holders and promote sustainable ecosystem growth.

* * *

### Conclusion[](https://docs.unisat.io/home/production-roadmap/overview-of-unisat-2025-work-priorities#conclusion)

This document shares a brief overview of our development plans for 2025. As our work progresses, we will update this plan periodically. In the coming year, we will focus on integrating development and operational resources more effectively, serving Bitcoin users more holistically, and fostering a more open industry atmosphere. We aim to collaborate more actively with different teams and communities, explore innovative ideas, contribute more open-source code, and organize more community activities, creating greater value and opportunities for users and developers.

_(This document will be updated as work progresses, Last Update: 2025-01-05)_

Last updated 11 months ago

# Get UTXO Info | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/open-api-documentation/api-for-fractal-bitcoin/general/transactions/get-utxo-info.md)
.

Copy

On this page

get

https://open-api-fractal.unisat.ioMainnet endpoint for public.

/v1/indexer/utxo/{txid}/{index}

Get the utxo info

Authorizations

bearerAuth

AuthorizationstringRequired

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Path parameters

txidstringRequired

Tx id

indexstringRequired

Tx id

Responses

200

Successful operation

application/json

codeinteger · enumOptionalPossible values: `0``-1`

msgstringOptionalExample: `OK`

dataobjectOptional

Show properties

401

Invalid API Key

get/v1/indexer/utxo/{txid}/{index}

HTTP

Copy

    GET /v1/indexer/utxo/{txid}/{index} HTTP/1.1
    Host: open-api-fractal.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*
    

Test it

200

Successful operation

Copy

    {
      "code": 0,
      "msg": "OK",
      "data": {
        "txid": "text",
        "vout": 0,
        "satoshi": 10000,
        "scriptType": "text",
        "scriptPk": "text",
        "codeType": 1,
        "address": "text",
        "height": 1,
        "idx": 1,
        "inscriptions": [\
          {\
            "inscriptionId": "text",\
            "inscriptionNumber": 1,\
            "isBRC20": true,\
            "moved": true,\
            "offset": 1\
          }\
        ],
        "isOpInRBF": false,
        "isSpent": false
      }
    }

The return result of this UTXO interface will have three scenarios:

1.  When UTXO has not been spent, it will return the information of this UTXO, and `isSpent` will be set to false.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FuSPxPwkcPfWDHdbaTFW3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df003683b-e476-4a90-ae1d-e26a033966d6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b82bd243054948ce1d718d197b4a7527&sv=3)

1.  When UTXO has been spent, but the transaction that spent it has not yet been confirmed, it will return the information of this UTXO, and `isSpent` will be set to true.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FSKwNRjOCCIrmqDpARLxD%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db2c87db8-c991-44df-b45f-c29c21722b02&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e992d43aa2d1912a5e613b0fe8677f25&sv=3)

1.  When UTXO has been spent and the transacted spend has been confirmed, it will return null.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FIcx3pACMXZotUT1tAOEa%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db409dd50-302e-4989-b3c1-5ae2e4e1eb4f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9e24e7102fb318f9da14a1026e635154&sv=3)

Last updated 1 year ago

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Path parameters

txidstringRequired

Tx id

indexstringRequired

Tx id

Responses

200

Successful operation

application/json

codeinteger · enumOptionalPossible values: `0``-1`

msgstringOptionalExample: `OK`

dataobjectOptional

Show properties

401

Invalid API Key

get/v1/indexer/utxo/{txid}/{index}

HTTP

Copy

    GET /v1/indexer/utxo/{txid}/{index} HTTP/1.1
    Host: open-api.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*
    

Test it

200

Successful operation

Copy

    {
      "code": 0,
      "msg": "OK",
      "data": {
        "txid": "text",
        "vout": 0,
        "satoshi": 10000,
        "scriptType": "text",
        "scriptPk": "text",
        "codeType": 1,
        "address": "text",
        "height": 1,
        "idx": 1,
        "inscriptions": [\
          {\
            "inscriptionId": "text",\
            "inscriptionNumber": 1,\
            "isBRC20": true,\
            "moved": true,\
            "offset": 1\
          }\
        ],
        "isOpInRBF": false,
        "isSpent": false
      }
    }

The return result of this UTXO interface will have three scenarios:

1.  When UTXO has not been spent, it will return the information of this UTXO, and `isSpent` will be set to false.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FuSPxPwkcPfWDHdbaTFW3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df003683b-e476-4a90-ae1d-e26a033966d6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b82bd243054948ce1d718d197b4a7527&sv=3)

1.  When UTXO has been spent, but the transaction that spent it has not yet been confirmed, it will return the information of this UTXO, and `isSpent` will be set to true.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FSKwNRjOCCIrmqDpARLxD%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db2c87db8-c991-44df-b45f-c29c21722b02&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e992d43aa2d1912a5e613b0fe8677f25&sv=3)

1.  When UTXO has been spent and the transacted spend has been confirmed, it will return null.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FIcx3pACMXZotUT1tAOEa%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db409dd50-302e-4989-b3c1-5ae2e4e1eb4f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9e24e7102fb318f9da14a1026e635154&sv=3)

Last updated 2 years ago

# Enable the two-factor authentication | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/enable-the-two-factor-authentication.md)
.

Two-factor authentication (2FA) is an additional security feature that requires users to enter a six-digit verification code when logging into their accounts. This greatly enhances account security and effectively prevents unauthorized access.

For the safety of your account, we recommend setting up Two-factor authentication immediately after registering to provide an extra layer of protection.

**How to link Two-factor authentication？**

1.  Enter UniSat [Developer Center](https://developer.unisat.io/dashboard)
    , click on the avatar on the right side of the page and select '**2FA Setting**' from the dropdown menu.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F8btJoPCiSTK087n6H8rh%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc4366c70-2bfc-4045-b249-7517ab4efc36&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a575d17258dc02b05e85f8182375b838&sv=3)

1.  Before binding 2FA, you need to confirm your email to ensure the operation is authorized by you. Click '**Send Code**' on the page, input the verification code received in your email, and click '**Submit**' to enter the 2FA binding page.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FkDUfs8dgzOEewucbsQKY%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Db2d440d3-e647-4922-8b73-0829fe024cc2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c87f1d0d14b09efc621384c9e556186d&sv=3)

1.  Follow the instructions on the page to first download and install an **authentication app**.
    

iOS: Search **Google Authenticator** in the App Store

Android: Search **Google Authenticator** in Google Play or use [this download link](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.google.android.apps.authenticator2)

Then, use the authenticator app to scan the QR code on the page and enter the six-digit verification code in the authentication app.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FMyoW9mYM8f2RxjeJTsyI%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df5f204bc-a5df-4567-b6a9-9b74e9eb9e7a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a71a4f06bba466844fcffb91f8c5a815&sv=3)

1.  Now you are done with 2FA! And of course, you can also disable 2FA if needed.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FzitBAQCIznqimmviT8wp%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D470acec5-fcce-48d4-8cbe-84f2bc6cae68&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d4bc172d10c8474d6928788976c517fc&sv=3)

Last updated 2 years ago

# Plans | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/plans.md)
.

UniSat UniSat AI Kit can still be accessed without an API key. However, to ensure fair allocation of resources, we recommend including an API key in all requests. Requests made without a key, or with a free key, may face strict rate limits or even no response under high load.

Each pricing model offers the following plans: Free, Specialist, Enterprise, Custom, and Pay-as-you-go. Visit [https://developer.unisat.io/](https://developer.unisat.io/)
 and follow the on-page instructions to apply.

* * *

*   **Free**: Ideal for conducting research and high-load testing. Obtain a one-time key for your short-term project.
    

✔Rate Limit: 30,000 calls/month

✔Rate Limit: 5 calls/second

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252Foj7jUgxuwiW7xGCOgMwR%252F1.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9878cd24-e036-4d05-8ce0-a05d9ee5fa9b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6f79fd7d991491902954fdc39d46ea59&sv=3)

*   **Specialist**: Suitable for developers running larger scripts and blockchain startups with a growing user base.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252Fqnk0aw5ZUZ1FM7zCaitk%252F3.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfe5f1b08-f262-4522-bd9c-9bf5de61aa00&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1cb1e6d02039a4d2463da1586afd2e5c&sv=3)

*   **Enterprise**: Perfect for wallets, exchanges, and startups in their growth phase.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FfYQB5LBot1zOvPfvYXq5%252F4.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfcd65e49-154e-4022-b0dc-be892b166c83&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=a4033b2634c3f50c3d794e97ae51c14e&sv=3)

*   **Pay as you go**: Suitable for temporary high-demand needs with flexible usage.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FavmuULaJdIFd1zDfXH4j%252F5.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D38ee3bcb-7c0f-4d34-8a62-e1ea1e4cd7b4&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=48251f4cc061b7ca697d4387c001f2b0&sv=3)

**Custom**: To inquire about a custom API plan, please feel free to contact us at contact@unisat.io.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FXwEaxAOkEXchNH9LnkrN%252F6.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D04727a01-e3f5-4016-ad64-8ec65abf800a&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ec05b68a7bde72269cb38f0cf009f415&sv=3)

If your API exceed the current usage limit, you can upgrade your API quota, you can upgrade your API quota by clicking "Upgrade Plan" on the right side of your dashboard:[https://developer.unisat.io/dashboard/](https://developer.unisat.io/dashboard/)
.

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/how-to-acquire-a-unisat-api-key.md)
.

Previously, API keys were requested by contacting the UniSat team and applying via email. With the new Developer Center, you will need to register with your email and choose the appropriate plan.

Here are the steps to acquire a UniSat API Key:

*   Visit [UniSat website](https://unisat.io/)
     and click '🌐' in the homepage navigation bar. From the dropdown menu, select 'API Service' to access the detailed API plans page. Alternatively, you can directly click [here](https://unisat.io/plans)
     to view the API plans.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FDCfQcpvSozgZjfWY9QDr%252F7.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ded0209c1-751b-4945-9656-efc2fcfc052d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ffc633dfa187c6e8557b4c9956409152&sv=3)

*   Choose the plan that suits your needs and click '**Get Started**' under the detailed description of the plan to go to the Developer Center plan acquire page. Or you can click [https://developer.unisat.io/plan](https://developer.unisat.io/plan)
     to enter the Developer Center, or click on
    

> Enjoy 50% off when you choose Specialist or Enterprise CLI & MCP limited-time plans
> 
> Enjoy 20% off when you choose Specialist or Enterprise Base limited-time plans

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FOgpwjmwwMPazQNm8V32G%252F8.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D595119c6-d374-416c-99c1-c4fa89815959&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9533d3d96fd6e2e0a37ac25cc53a4407&sv=3)

*   Before acquiring a plan, you need to register a Developer Center account using your email.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FBlkwVnfSxnSD4tZOWYtX%252F20240716-205021.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D40744b9b-b4e3-49d0-bb93-9f5905579447&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3e3b572f8fcadf560c69d93d91b179b6&sv=3)

*   Once registered, go to the Dashboard page where you will see your API Key current API Key configuration, and your API Request Log.
    
    *   For newly registered accounts, we offer developers a free access quota of 5 calls/second, 1,000 calls/day on Bitcoin
        
    *   Plans for Fractal Bitcoin are free to use.
        
    *   API Keys for Bitcoin and Fractal are separate and cannot be used interchangeably. You'll need to apply for a Bitcoin API Key and a Fractal API Key separately.
        
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FUFBVf4qCjBy5y4HZwvvU%252F9.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D59ead6f1-eca6-47b7-ac34-57bbcb4cbba2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b1212d8981c14a9b1075659bea3f3f95&sv=3)

If you exceed the current usage limit, you can upgrade your API quota.

How do I upgrade my API plan? ​​

*   Click **"Upgrade Plan"** on the right side of your dashboard, or
    
*   Click **"Plan – Bitcoin Mainnet"** in the upper-left corner to open the API plan selection page and choose a higher-tier plan.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F4BhKL4y9Am001EgL3t4Y%252F11.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7336e8a0-7237-4f7d-9a79-b332e67badcc&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6a9f83b7c7255dbf97bd2fa270188f66&sv=3)

*   Choose the plan you need and click the '**Buy**' button under the specific plan to enter the acquire page.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FSPlQ7XFtzuzrfOpYy3qG%252F12.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D76a6bbd6-64ce-4f00-ba1c-d94fbf5539e8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=eb7f1060aacb78339f20a93fe84d8fb6&sv=3)

*   Payments are currently accepted via PayPal, BTC, and USDT(ETH & TRON).
    

Here is an example of a BTC payment method, click '**Pay with BTC**', then select '**Pay with Wallet**'. The page will prompt you to sign; confirm the input and output information carefully, and click 'Sign & Pay' to complete the acquire.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252Fs2W4xztvbNgJqq3eTt1C%252F13.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D45f110e4-e422-47b6-99c9-a7e56ddb02db&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c1cabbe63cd98288aafa6c157173c1d1&sv=3)

*   After the acquire is complete, you can view your plan acquire record on the **Billing** page.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F4XVw8Laiepe4ivrjLIG9%252F14.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deda03246-4158-4caa-8bf8-0a285a62c67c&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=639ad553e0ca2e2b16c122489072bea5&sv=3)

# API Terms Of Use | UniSat

These API Terms of Use (the “Terms” or “Agreement”) govern your access to and use of the application programming interfaces, related documentation, code, tools, and data services (collectively, the “API” or the “Service”) made available by UniPro Technology Limited and/or its affiliates (“UniSat,” “we,” “us,” or “our”). By accessing or using the API, you (the “Developer” or “User”) agree to be bound by these Terms. If you are using the API on behalf of a company or other legal entity, you represent that you have the authority to bind such entity to these Terms.

**1\. Definitions.**

“API” means (i) any machine-accessible interface provided by UniSat that enables access to UniSat Offerings, (ii) any sample code, libraries, or tools provided by UniSat, and (iii) any associated documentation.

“Open-Source Components” means any software, libraries, or tools provided by UniSat that are licensed under a separate, publicly available open-source license (such as the MIT License) and are accessible independent of the API.

“UniSat Offerings” means the website [https://unisat.io](https://unisat.io/)
 and any other software, products, data, or platforms offered by UniSat.

“Application” means any software application, website, tool, service, or product created or offered by you that uses the API.

“API Content” means Your use of the API and display of any data or content accessed via the API.

“Developer Documentation” means the technical documentation, usage guidelines, call volume limits, and other materials we provide or make available to you.

“Confidential Information” means you may receive access to non-public technical information, specifications, or data relating to the UniSat API.

“Term” means the period beginning on the date you first accept these Terms and ending upon termination in accordance with Section 12.

**2\. Scope of Agreement.**

2.1 This Agreement governs your access to and use of the UniSat API, including any data, content, or services made available through the API (collectively, “API Services”).

2.2 Any open-source software, libraries, sample code, or tools that are provided by UniSat under a separate open-source license (e.g., MIT License) and are accessible outside the context of the API (“Open-Source Components”) are not subject to the terms of this Agreement.

2.3 Your use of any Open-Source Components is governed solely by the terms of the applicable open-source license accompanying such components. The disclaimers of warranty and limitations of liability set forth in this Agreement do not apply to Open-Source Components to the extent they are governed by such open-source licenses. Notwithstanding the foregoing, Section 5.4 (Open-Source Code) of this Agreement shall remain applicable.

**3\. Access and Eligibility.**

3.1 Registration. You must register for an account to access certain features of the API. You are responsible for all activities conducted under your account credentials.

3.2 Eligibility. You represent and warrant that:

(a) You are of legal age and have the capacity to enter into a binding contract.

(b) You and your end users are not located in, or a citizen or resident of, any jurisdiction where the use of the Service is prohibited or requires special authorization.

3.3 We reserve the right, at our sole discretion, to construe, limit, and modify the scope of Service in any jurisdiction at any time and to determine, at our sole discretion, your eligibility to access or use this Agreement and the Service. You acknowledge that such determinations may affect your ability to access or use the Service and agree to bear all associated risks and consequences. If you fail to meet such eligibility requirements, we may, temporarily or permanently, suspend, restrict or terminate your access to the Service.

**4\. License and Use.**

4.1 Limited License. Subject to these Terms, UniSat grants you a limited, non-exclusive, non-transferable, non-sublicensable, and revocable license to: (i) access and use the API solely to develop, test, and operate your Application; and (ii) use the related documentation.

4.3 Developer Documentation. Your use of the API Content must comply with the Developer Documentation. The Developer Documentation is incorporated into these Terms by reference. In the event of any conflict between the Developer Documentation and these Terms, these Terms shall govern.

**6\. Developer Responsibilities.**

12.1 Term. This Agreement commences on the date you first accept it and continues until terminated in accordance with this Section 12.

You agree to protect it using at least the same degree of care you use for your own confidential information of similar importance.

**14\. Governing Law.**

This Agreement, your use of the Sites and the Services, and any dispute, controversy, difference or claim arising out of or relating to this Agreement, or your use of the Sites and the Services, including the existence, validity, interpretation, performance, breach or termination of this Agreement or any dispute regarding non-contractual obligations arising out of or relating to them (“Dispute”) shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Hong Kong, without giving effect to any conflict of laws principles.

**15\. Dispute Resolution and Class Action Waivers.**

15.1 Any Dispute shall be first resolved through good faith negotiations. You must inform us of the Dispute by sending an email to contact legal @unisat.io. If no settlement is reached within sixty (60) days of our first email to commence the negotiations, the Dispute shall be resolved exclusively by arbitration in accordance with Section15.2 below.

15.2 Any Dispute shall be referred to and finally resolved by arbitration administered by the Hong Kong International Arbitration Centre (HKIAC) in accordance with the HKIAC Administered Arbitration Rules in force when the Notice of Arbitration is submitted. The law of this arbitration clause shall be Hong Kong law. The number of arbitrators shall be one. The arbitration proceedings shall be conducted in English. The fees and expenses of the arbitral tribunal shall be determined on the basis of \[Schedule 2/ Schedule 3\] of these Rules. The arbitration shall take place in Hong Kong, unless both you and us mutually agree on an alternative location.

15.3 Class Action Waiver. YOU AND WE AGREE THAT EACH MAY BRING CLAIMS AGAINST THE OTHER ONLY ON AN INDIVIDUAL BASIS, AND NOT AS A PLAINTIFF OR CLASS MEMBER IN ANY PURPORTED CLASS OR REPRESENTATIVE PROCEEDING. YOU AND WE EXPRESSLY WAIVE ANY RIGHT TO FILE A CLASS ACTION OR SEEK RELIEF ON A CLASS BASIS. Unless both you and we agree, no arbitrator or judge may consolidate more than one person’s claims or otherwise preside over any form of a representative or class proceeding. The arbitrator may award injunctive relief only in favor of the individual party seeking relief and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by that party’s individual claim. If a court decides that applicable law precludes enforcement of any of this paragraph’s limitations as to a particular claim for relief, then that claim (and only that claim) must be severed from the arbitration and may be brought in court. If any court or arbitrator determines that the class action waiver set forth in this paragraph is void or unenforceable for any reason or that an arbitration can proceed on a class basis, then the arbitration provision set forth above shall be deemed null and void in its entirety and the parties shall be deemed to have not agreed to arbitrate disputes.

**16\. General Terms.**

16.1 Modifications. We may update these Terms from time to time. The updated version will be posted online. Your continued use of the API after the effective date constitutes acceptance of the revised Terms.

16.2 Severability. If any provision of these Terms is held invalid, the remaining provisions shall remain in full force and effect.

Copy

On this page

Please note that access to this API requires the API key to have **brc20-swap** permissions enabled.

### Select the tick information that you can use based on the address.[](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/open-api-documentation/api-for-bitcoin/brc20-swap/get-address-all-ticker#get-v1-brc20-swap-select)

get

https://open-api.unisat.ioMainnet endpoint for public

/v1/brc20-swap/select

Authorizations

bearerAuth

AuthorizationstringRequired

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Query parameters

addressstringRequired

searchstringOptional

Fuzzy matching

Responses

200

Default Response

application/json

codenumberRequired

msgstringRequired

dataobject\[\]Required

Show properties

get/v1/brc20-swap/select

HTTP

Copy

    GET /v1/brc20-swap/select?address=text HTTP/1.1
    Host: open-api.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*
    

Test it

200

Default Response

Copy

    {
      "code": 1,
      "msg": "text",
      "data": [\
        {\
          "tick": "text",\
          "decimal": "text",\
          "brc20Balance": "text",\
          "swapBalance": "text"\
        }\
      ]
    }

get

Authorizations

bearerAuth

AuthorizationstringRequired

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Query parameters

addressstringRequired

tickstringRequired

Responses

200

Default Response

application/json

codenumberRequired

msgstringRequired

dataobjectRequired

Show properties

get/v1/brc20-swap/balance

HTTP

Copy

    Host: open-api.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*

# Get Address BRC-20 Ticker Info | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/open-api-documentation/api-for-bitcoin/brc-20/get-address-brc-20-ticker-info.md)
.

Copy

On this page

get

https://open-api.unisat.ioMainnet endpoint for public

/v1/indexer/address/{address}/brc20/{ticker}/info

Obtain BRC20 token infomation by address, including available balance, transferable balance, number of transferable inscriptions, the first few Inscriptions, etc.

Authorizations

bearerAuth

AuthorizationstringRequired

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Path parameters

addressstringRequired

Address

tickerstringRequired

Token ticker

Responses

200

Successful operation

application/json

codeinteger · enumOptionalPossible values: `0``-1`

msgstringOptionalExample: `OK`

dataobjectOptional

Show properties

401

Invalid API Key

get/v1/indexer/address/{address}/brc20/{ticker}/info

HTTP

Copy

    GET /v1/indexer/address/{address}/brc20/{ticker}/info HTTP/1.1
    Host: open-api.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*
    

Test it

200

Successful operation

Copy

    {
      "code": 0,
      "msg": "OK",
      "data": {
        "ticker": "text",
        "overallBalance": "text",
        "availableBalance": "text",
        "availableBalanceSafe": "text",
        "availableBalanceUnSafe": "text",
        "transferableBalance": "text",
        "historyCount": 1,
        "historyInscriptions": [\
          {\
            "confirmations": 1,\
            "data": {\
              "amt": "text",\
              "decimal": "text",\
              "lim": "text",\
              "max": "text",\
              "minted": "text",\
              "op": "text",\
              "tick": "text",\
              "to": "text"\
            },\
            "inscriptionId": "text",\
            "inscriptionNumber": 1\
          }\
        ],
        "transferableCount": 1,
        "transferableInscriptions": [\
          {\
            "confirmations": 1,\
            "data": {\
              "amt": "text",\
              "decimal": "text",\
              "lim": "text",\
              "max": "text",\
              "minted": "text",\
              "op": "text",\
              "tick": "text",\
              "to": "text"\
            },\
            "inscriptionId": "text",\
            "inscriptionNumber": 1\
          }\
        ]
      }
    }

# Get Fractal Circulating Supply | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/open-api-documentation/api-for-fractal-bitcoin/get-fractal-circulating-supply.md)
.

Copy

On this page

Public endpoints don't require authentication.

### Get supply FB in fractal mainnet[](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/open-api-documentation/api-for-fractal-bitcoin/get-fractal-circulating-supply#get-v1-public-fractal-supply)

get

https://open-api-fractal.unisat.ioMainnet endpoint for public.

/v1/public/fractal/supply

Authorizations

bearerAuth

AuthorizationstringRequired

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Responses

200

Successful operation

application/json

codeinteger · int32Optional

msgstringOptionalExample: `OK`

dataobject · FractalSupplyOptional

Show properties

401

Invalid API Key

get/v1/public/fractal/supply

HTTP

Copy

    GET /v1/public/fractal/supply HTTP/1.1
    Host: open-api-fractal.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*
    

Test it

200

Successful operation

Copy

    {
      "code": 1,
      "msg": "OK",
      "data": {
        "blocks": 1,
        "supply": 1
      }
    }

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FUt1NbRSNFmVmpx2sQ8YN%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D83b4014d-fb6e-42c5-9311-9b5f19469d29&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6d009a50e7914b3d065af4251f762ac7&sv=3)

*   blockheight = blocks-1. So the current height is 5578
    
*   The supply unit is `satoshi` . 119819188621300 = 1198191.88621300 FB
    
*   [https://open-api-fractal.unisat.io/v1/public/fractal/supply?format=text](https://open-api-fractal.unisat.io/v1/indexer/fractal/total-supply?format=text)
     will return text format with FB unit
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FaVbkjYd1JUUpmRnQTNc3%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd3360637-0e39-4002-8895-00516ebf0731&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=23f57eff79c0bbc8292075c1a5116e51&sv=3)

### Apply for a UniSat API Key[](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/unisat-ai-kit#apply-for-a-unisat-api-key)

Before you start using UniSat API, you need to apply for a UniSat API Key.

We offer several plans: Free, Specialist, Enterprise, Custom, and Pay-as-you-go. Visit [https://developer.unisat.io/](https://developer.unisat.io/)
 and follow the on-page instructions to apply.

How to Acquire a UniSat API Key: [https://docs.unisat.io/dev-center/developer-center/how-to-acquire-a-unisat-api-key](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/how-to-acquire-a-unisat-api-key)

Note: UniSat API can still be accessed without an API key. However, to ensure fair allocation of resources, we recommend including an API key in all requests. Requests made without a key, or with a free key, may face strict rate limits or even no response under high load.

* * *

### Usage[](https://docs.unisat.io/developer-support/unisat-ai-kit#usage)

1.  Use the Swagger UI [https://open-api.unisat.io](https://open-api.unisat.io/)
     and read the documents [https://github.com/unisat-wallet/unisat-dev-docs](https://github.com/unisat-wallet/unisat-dev-docs)
     to test API calls.
    
2.  Register at [https://developer.unisat.io/](https://developer.unisat.io/)
     and authenticate as required by each API.
    
3.  Handle error codes and messages according to the [error codes documentation](https://github.com/unisat-wallet/unisat-dev-docs/blob/master/errors/README.md)
    

* * *

For any questions, support, or discussions, please reach out to us on Discord: [http://discord.gg/unisat](http://discord.gg/unisat)

Thank you for using UniSat Open API!

# Simple Bridge | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge.md)
.

Simple Bridge, developed by UniSat, is a small service designed to securely and efficiently connect assets from different protocols to InSwap. By integrating the latest cryptographic algorithms, Simple Bridge enhances the security and performance of cross-chain asset transfers, bringing innovation to the blockchain ecosystem.

#### Key Features:[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge#key-features)

*   **Multisig with Anonymous Validators:** Simple Bridge secures transactions using a multi-signature system distributed across anonymous validator nodes. Each transaction is signed randomly by a subset of these nodes, increasing fault tolerance and making it harder for attackers to compromise the bridge.
    
*   **End-to-End Encrypted Communication:** All transaction data is transmitted using strong end-to-end encryption. Even if the data is intercepted, it cannot be deciphered—ensuring users' funds and information remain secure at all times.
    
*   **Distributed Service Architecture:** The service architecture of Simple Bridge is designed to be distributed, exposing only necessary nodes and minimizing potential security risks. This design makes the system more robust while improving overall operational efficiency.
    

#### Supported Assets[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge#supported-assets)

*   At launch, Simple Bridge supports the following assets:sBTC, sORDI, sSATS, sQUAQ, sOSHI, sPEPE, sTRAC, sMEME, sRATS, sPIZZA
    
*   All bridged assets carry the **s-** prefix, signifying their status as cross-chain assets transferred via Simple Bridge.
    

#### Naming Conventions[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge#naming-conventions)

To maintain consistency and ensure easy identification, Simple Bridge follows a specific naming convention for bridged assets:

*   Assets are prefixed with s-, indicating they are bridged through Simple Bridge.
    
*   For assets whose names are fewer than six characters, underscores ("\_") are used as padding to maintain consistency. For instance:
    
    *   sBTC is represented as sBTC\_\_\_000
        
    

This naming system ensures that all bridged assets meet a uniform format, making them easier to identify and manage.

#### Bridge Fees[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge#bridge-fees)

Using Simple Bridge incurs the following fees:

*   **Deposit Fee**: 0.00003 BTC
    
*   **Withdrawal Fee**: 0.25 FB
    

Simple Bridge will become an important tool for secure and efficient cross-chain transfers on Fractal Bitcoin, offering unmatched security, scalability, and ease of use. Backed by the expertise of the UniSat team, it provides a solid foundation for innovation and applications on Fractal Bitcoin.

Last updated 11 months ago

On this page

Get the orders associated with your API-KEY

get

/v2/inscribe/order/list

Get order list of current apikey

Authorizations

bearerAuth

AuthorizationstringRequired

Bearer authentication header of the form Bearer <token>.

Query parameters

cursorintegerRequired

Start offset

Example: `0`

sizeintegerRequired

Number of items returned

Example: `16`

sortstring · enumOptional

Sort by (asc/desc)

Example: `asc`Possible values: `asc``desc`

statusstring · enumOptional

Status of order

Possible values: `pending``inscribing``minted``closed``refunded`

receiveAddressstringOptional

ReceiveAddress of order

clientIdstringOptional

ClientId of order

Responses

200

Successful operation

application/json

codeinteger · enumOptionalPossible values: `0``-1`

msgstringOptionalExample: `OK`

dataobjectOptional

Show properties

401

Invalid API Key

get/v2/inscribe/order/list

HTTP

Copy

    GET /v2/inscribe/order/list?cursor=1&size=1 HTTP/1.1
    Host: open-api-fractal.unisat.io
    Authorization: Bearer YOUR_SECRET_TOKEN
    Accept: */*
    

Test it

200

Successful operation

Copy

    {
      "code": 0,
      "msg": "OK",
      "data": {
        "detail": [\
          {\
            "orderId": "",\
            "status": "pending",\
            "payAddress": "",\
            "receiveAddress": "",\
            "amount": 3000,\
            "paidAmount": 1,\
            "outputValue": 1,\
            "feeRate": 1,\
            "minerFee": 1,\
            "serviceFee": 1,\
            "devFee": 1,\
            "files": [\
              {\
                "filename": "10000.sats",\
                "inscriptionId": "",\
                "status": "pending"\
              }\
            ],\
            "count": 1,\
            "pendingCount": 1,\
            "unconfirmedCount": 0,\
            "confirmedCount": 0,\
            "createTime": 1693439128100,\
            "refundTxid": "",\
            "refundAmount": 1,\
            "refundFeeRate": 1\
          }\
        ],
        "start": 1,
        "total": 1
      }
    }

*   status (Optional) : pending/inscribing/minted/closed/refunded
    
*   receiveAddress (Optional): Filter by receive address
    
*   clientId (Optional): The clientId passed when creating an order is useful for filtering a certain user's orders.

# Get Order List | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
