# How to use the Simple Bridge | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge.md)
.

We’re excited to announce that Simple Bridge is live on the Fractal Bitcoin Mainnet! With Simple Bridge, you can securely and efficiently connect assets from different protocols to InSwap and enjoy seamless cross-chain transfers.

Start using it here: [https://fractal.unisat.io/bridge](https://fractal.unisat.io/bridge)

* * *

### **Understand Simple Bridge Rules**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#understand-simple-bridge-rules)

Before you begin, make sure to familiarize yourself with the Simple Bridge rules.

**Supported Assets:**

*   sBTC, sORDI, sSATS, sQUAQ, sOSHI, sPEPE, sTRAC, sMEME, sRATS, sPIZZA
    
*   All bridged assets carry the **s-** prefix, indicating their status as cross-chain assets transferred via Simple Bridge.
    

**Bridge Fees:**

*   Deposit Fee: 0.00003 BTC
    
*   Withdrawal Fee: 0.25 FB
    

* * *

### **Setting Up Your Fractal Bitcoin Wallet**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#setting-up-your-fractal-bitcoin-wallet)

*   Open the UniSat Wallet extension. You can download it by clicking [here](https://fractal.unisat.io/download)
    .
    

### **Using Simple Bridge**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#using-simple-bridge)

#### **Step 1: Access the Simple Bridge Page**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#step-1-access-the-simple-bridge-page)

*   Go to Simple Bridge Page [https://fractal.unisat.io/bridge](https://fractal.unisat.io/bridge) and click "Connect" in the top-right corner to sign in with your wallet.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FftcpkZrNsBq45Or00Gtq%252F1.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D03f341bf-c55d-4dcb-9485-6268f5815ee9&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5e9bd7acb62349529f0dce047f37b558&sv=3)

#### Step 2: Bridge BTC or Inscribe Transfer for brc-20[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#step-2-bridge-btc-or-inscribe-transfer-for-brc-20)

*   For BTC: Select BTC and simply input the amount you wish to bridge in the "You Pay" section.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252Fq9vn8pCBvaowMWQK3J3e%252F2.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D825addfa-0f34-4227-8c39-13d52294f04b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e739628b2c31e7c2c7d3195bca27a639&sv=3)

*   For brc-20: First, you need to perform an Inscribe Transfer. Select the ticker you want to bridge from the dropdown menu next to the ticker name, then choose a transfer to bridge.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252Fpb4NVqoMXFh7MR4TyQpi%252F5.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7af0ce00-4db2-463a-9089-57280c9d7bcc&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=767bc5c69aebadbf9f98f2b0c00422c6&sv=3)

If you haven't completed an Inscribe Transfer yet, follow the steps below:

*   In the "You Pay" section, select the ticker you wish to bridge and click + to start the Inscribe Transfer.
    
*   In the pop-up signature window, enter the Amount, Output Value, and Fee Rate. After verifying the information is correct, click "Next."
    
*   Confirm the signature details, and click "Next" again.
    
*   Double-check the inputs and outputs in the signature window. Once everything is correct, click "Sign & Pay" to complete the Inscribe Transfer.
    
*   After inscribe transfer, you need to patiently wait for 3 block confirmations.
    
*   Make sure the amount for the Inscribe Transfer is not less than the minimum value specified on the interface.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F0MvwlSda4Z2H3UMX51kc%252F3.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D2459fc01-22ab-4d5b-ae3d-d78fdd6af568&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=966baa0c4a8ccbe789af6b2ba9b85610&sv=3)

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F615rmHnsy10tUxkMwol4%252F4.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D20b3f69e-a79c-4614-94c4-f5af6e7ae18c&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f1122eadce49191d27f16cb571a0666e&sv=3)

#### **Step 3: Bridge Operation**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#step-3-bridge-operation)

1.  In the Simple Bridge page, select the asset you want to bridge and input the amount in the You Pay field.
    
2.  The system will automatically calculate and display the amount you will receive in the You Receive field. Verify the Receiving Address and Fee. If everything is correct, click Deposit.
    
3.  In the signature window, confirm the inputs and outputs, then click Sign to complete the operation.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F9TrEoK7zwdAMwA1ZPU4d%252F6.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dacb7676b-bb24-42d3-ab2a-9e44ec1a8366&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d074e706dc3195f3c8a02e1147802165&sv=3)

**Step 4: View Bridge Details**

*   After signing, you'll be taken to the Bridge Details page. The system will automatically complete the bridge operation.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F7j3mOT12dWCch4PEx1R2%252FSim.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D433549fa-37dc-459e-9ccf-867ef7b433de&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=8f233e01492475edf09c1ed9cbfbde18&sv=3)

### Withdraw from Simple Bridge[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#withdraw-from-simple-bridge)

The operations for withdrawing and depositing through the bridge are the same.

First, click the 🔁 icon on the page to switch to withdraw mode.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FExgdZPRwUm1vwKqZW945%252F102.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7c285ed2-7014-493f-8828-4929aff6495f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e25dde6f5a84f29e554c6936bb66934f&sv=3)

#### **Step 1: Inscribe Transfer**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#step-1-inscribe-transfer)

*   First, you need to perform an Inscribe Transfer. Select the ticker you want to withdraw from the dropdown menu next to the ticker name, then choose a transfer to withdraw.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252F52jjg0NBXHZqQ8UFnWn7%252F8.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8c366651-d486-4be5-bf5f-f39f1c838ff2&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=09582a928582865b2c562b79a342f9a8&sv=3)

*   If you haven't completed an Inscribe Transfer yet, follow the steps below:In the "You Pay" section, select the ticker you wish to withdraw and click + to perform the Inscribe Transfer.
    
*   In the pop-up signature window, enter the Amount, Output Value, and Fee Rate. After verifying the information is correct, click "Next."
    
*   Confirm the signature details, and click "Next" again.
    
*   Double-check the inputs and outputs in the signature window. Once everything is correct, click "Sign & Pay" to complete the Inscribe Transfer.
    
*   After inscribe transfer, you need to patiently wait for 3 block confirmations.
    
*   Make sure the amount for the Inscribe Transfer is not less than the minimum value specified on the interface.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FqVjG55xe9CFpwqqhe76x%252F9.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9ba31a33-8e01-49ae-84ae-176eb3a03edd&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5d33c4fb585d370a89b92dfe7595d1dd&sv=3)

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FUzA3Oe7fCE0H2ERujM21%252F101.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3De10f924f-fb1e-4236-8cdf-3d061ed8a2bc&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e27d5de3b92ab6205b686fac0016d5bb&sv=3)

#### Step 2: Withdraw Operation[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#step-2-withdraw-operation)

1.  In the "You Pay" section, select the ticker you want to withdraw, and click the amount you previously Inscribe Transferred.
    
2.  The system will auto-fill the "You Receive" field with the corresponding amount. Double-check the Receiving Address and Fee. Once confirmed, click "Withdraw."
    
3.  In the signature window, confirm the inputs and outputs, then click "Sign" to complete the process.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FCE8kRnZDh6HEHbY6neLP%252F107.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D0762fc8f-1622-4ce7-826d-a82fa7148460&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=cee5dece4460fc1668a8b9d95c437b90&sv=3)

#### **Step 3: View Withdraw Details**[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#step-3-view-withdraw-details)

*   After signing, you'll be redirected to the Withdraw Bridge Details page. The system will automatically complete the bridge operation.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252Fwaoy5KB6S0FhoZimN85x%252F109.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Df908bd00-c3c8-4a4a-81ad-957978d1c381&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=caed2fb6613d5dc39f04ecb5eb7c5fab&sv=3)

### View Bridge History[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/deprecated/simple-bridge/how-to-use-the-simple-bridge#view-bridge-history)

You can view your Bridge Deposit and Withdraw history anytime by visiting the Bridge History page: [https://fractal.unisat.io/bridge/history](https://fractal.unisat.io/bridge/history)
.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FGt6xVCnKoLvSztuFuEGK%252F111.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7c8c8047-4de1-47ec-b19d-748da24240f8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=66135690e4ab5ca376a85fbccfdc6ca8&sv=3)

# FB Farming Legal Disclaimer | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/legal/fb-farming-legal-disclaimer.md)
.

UniSat ( “we” or “us”) provides the FB Farming / Staking feature (“Staking Feature”) to reward ecosystem users and promote sustainable growth of the Fractal Bitcoin ecosystem. Users should carefully read, understand, and accept this disclaimer and related terms before participating.

1.  **No Investment Advice**
    

This staking feature is part of product functionality and ecosystem incentives. It does not constitute any form of investment advice, financial commitment, or guaranteed returns. Participation is at the user’s own discretion and based on their risk tolerance.

1.  **Reward Structure and Risk of Forfeiture**
    

  a. Rewards are determined by multiple factors, including base APY, Tier Bonuses, Gem boosts, etc. Some rewards are only granted upon fulfilling specific criteria (e.g., staking duration, Gem expiry, etc.).

  b. If a user unstakes early (before completing the chosen staking duration), certain bonus rewards may be forfeited. The base APY will be prorated, but tier-based staking rewards and Gem boost rewards can only be obtained upon completion of the full staking period.

1.  **Market and Value Volatility Risk**
    

  a. The market price of FB may rise or fall. During the staking period, if price depreciation of FB exceeds the value of rewards earned, user’s net asset value may be reduced.

  b. Rewards when converted, traded, or withdrawn may incur losses due to market prices, liquidity issues, transaction fees, or slippage.

1.  **Reward Pool Capacity and Staking Limits**
    

  a. Some staking or bonus farming pools have a maximum reward allocation (e.g., Pool 1 with 1,000,000 FB). Early participants may consume a large portion of rewards, leaving limited or no rewards for later participants.  

b. Minimum or maximum staking amounts per address / wallet may apply. Exceeding or not meeting these limits may result in inability to stake as expected or reward calculations being different.

1.  **Technical and System Risks**
    

  Although FB Farming utilizes Taproot scripts, the UTXO model, and a non-custodial design for enhanced security, technical upgrades, protocol changes, third-party factors, or force majeure events may still lead to modifications, interruptions, or termination of the staking program.

1.  **Compliance and Taxation Obligations**
    

  a. Users must ensure their participation complies with applicable laws and regulations in their jurisdiction.  

b. Any taxes or obligations arising from reward earnings are the sole responsibility of the user. We are not liable for any tax consequences.

1.  **User Operation Risk**
    

  a. Users may make mistakes during staking, adding Gems, selecting staking duration, or unstaking, which may result in loss of certain rewards or assets.  

b. Before any such operation, user should carefully read on-screen warnings, risk alert boxes, and all relevant terms.

1.  **Limitation of Liability**
    

We shall not be liable for any losses of asset value, reduced or delayed rewards, interruption or termination of service, or failure to attain expected returns caused by market volatility, system or network failure, protocol changes, third-party actions, force majeure, or any other cause. In no event shall UniSat be liable to you for any loss of profits, revenue, goodwill, business opportunities, or for any indirect, incidental, special, consequential, or punitive damages arising out of or related to your participation in the staking program.

1.  **Right to Modify, Suspend, or Terminate and Final Interpretation**
    

We reserve the right to modify, suspend or terminate the staking feature or related activities at any time (including but not limited to changes to reward structures, duration, Gem conditions, APY etc.), and retain the final right of interpretation over this feature and its activities.

[PreviousDeveloper Service Legal Disclaimer](https://docs.unisat.io/legal/developer-service-legal-disclaimer)

# Fractal Bitcoin One Year Anniversary Carnival  Legal Disclaimer | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/legal/fractal-bitcoin-one-year-anniversary-carnival-legal-disclaimer.md)
.

**Legal Disclaimer**The Fractal Bitcoin One Year Anniversary Carnival (the “Carnival”) is a goodwill gesture by the Fractal Bitcoin project team (the “Team”) to express appreciation to our community. Participation in this event is subject to the following disclaimer:

1.  **No Investment or Financial Advice** The Carnival does not constitute an offer to sell, a solicitation of an offer to buy, or a recommendation of any kind regarding any financial product or digital asset. Nothing in connection with the Carnival should be construed as investment, financial, legal, or tax advice.
    
2.  **Eligibility and Allocation** Eligibility criteria and allocation details are determined solely at the discretion of the Team and may be modified, suspended, or cancelled at any time.
    
3.  **No Warranties** FB are provided “as is” without warranties of any kind, express or implied, including but not limited to merchantability, fitness for a particular purpose, or non-infringement. The Team makes no guarantee of value, usability, or availability of FB, either now or in the future.
    
4.  **Regulatory and Jurisdictional Restrictions** Participation may be restricted or prohibited in certain jurisdictions. It is the sole responsibility of participants to ensure compliance with applicable laws, regulations, and restrictions in their respective locations.
    
5.  **Assumption of Risk** By participating, you acknowledge and accept the inherent risks of blockchain-based systems, including but not limited to technological issues, system vulnerabilities, regulatory uncertainties, and potential changes in access conditions. The Team shall not be responsible for failures caused by network issues, system errors, third-party services, or other factors beyond its reasonable control.
    
6.  **No Guarantee of Future Benefits** Any anniversary badges, recognitions, or associated features are symbolic in nature. While they may provide access to certain community initiatives, the Team provides no guarantee as to their utility, duration, or value.
    
7.  **Limitation of Liability** In no event shall the Team be liable to you for any loss of profits, revenue, goodwill, or for any indirect, incidental, special, consequential, or punitive damages arising out of or related to participation in the Carnival.
    

**By participating in the Carnival, you acknowledge that you have read, understood, and agreed to this Legal Disclaimer.**

# How to Transfer $BABY to a Cosmos Wallet | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/events/babylon-staking-activity/how-to-transfer-usdbaby-to-a-cosmos-wallet.md)
.

If you previously used UniSat Wallet to participate in Babylon’s staking, you can transfer your $BABY airdrop to a Cosmos wallet for easier management and further operations.

This guide is for reference only. For more details about $BABY, please refer to the official Babylon website: [https://babylonlabs.io/](https://babylonlabs.io/)

**Step 1: Prepare a Cosmos Wallet**

*   Choose a Cosmos-compatible wallet that supports $BABY, such as Leap or Keplr.
    
*   After creating your wallet account, switch the network to Babylon Network.
    
*   Click on Receive, and copy your BBN address.
    

(This example uses Leap wallet.)

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FHx0gxIc2otI8GCNQjlBe%252Fleap.gif%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D78a65b0d-96d0-4332-a36d-3adf73f0e00b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ea2c12cc01617d7ca330e2ec8abbfaea&sv=3)

**Step 2: Open Your UniSat Wallet**

*   Open your UniSat Wallet, and switch to the **BTC Mainnet network**.
    
*   Go to the Discover page, and find the **Babylon** entry.
    

Ensure that your UniSat Extension Wallet is updated to the latest version v1.6.1. Click the link to download or update: [https://unisat.io/download](https://unisat.io/download)

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FD8PQsKARKUmx9Oo7nlnm%252F1.PNG%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcb5b0457-0e0c-4489-bf98-99e6f618e81b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ef52fadd2bf99ccd65a4c39afc29b8ec&sv=3)

**Step 3: Go to the Babylon Page**

*   On the Babylon page, you will see the Claimed BABY section. Click the **Transfer** button in the Claimed BABY section.
    
*   Follow the on-screen prompts to click **Transfer $BABY to Cosmos Wallet**, then click **Confirm** to confirm your action.
    
*   In the transfer interface, input the BBN wallet address you created in Step 1 as the receiving address for your $BABY, and enter the transfer amount.
    
*   Once you confirm the information is correct, click Next, then sign the transaction to complete the transfer of your $BABY to your Cosmos wallet address.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FcltHR24FOAXOJew3VMbG%252Fcosmos.gif%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dd7a258e4-160b-493a-91b9-7fa7860238e0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=f28baa0b72b4c12f17d7fb8d417729aa&sv=3)

**Additional Notes:**

*   Ensure that your Cosmos wallet is set up to support $BABY transfers and is connected to the Babylon Network.
    
*   If the Babylon DApp entry is not showing in your UniSat wallet, please ensure your wallet is updated to the latest version.
    
*   Babylon is a third-party project. Please make sure you fully understand the associated risks and terms before processing.
    
*   If you encounter any issues, please reach out to us via our Discord: [http://discord.gg/unisat](http://discord.gg/unisat)
    

* * *

**FAQ**

**Q: I previously used UniSat Wallet to participate in Babylon BTC staking. How can I register for the BTC staking registration now?** A: The BTC staking registration on April 10 is only for addresses that participated in Babylon BTC Staking Phase Cap 1. If you used UniSat Wallet to stake BTC during Cap 1, you can go directly to the Babylon official website for more information.

If you participated in Babylon BTC staking Cap 2 or Cap 3, please stay tuned for future updates from the Babylon team.

* * *

**Q: When can I use UniSat Wallet to join Babylon Bitcoin Staking Phase 2?** A: Please stay tuned for more updates — we’ll keep you informed as soon as it’s live.

* * *

**Q: How do I access the Babylon website in UniSat Wallet?** A: Simply open the latest version of the UniSat Extension, go to Dapp Center, and tap on Babylon to jump straight to the site.

Download the latest UniSat Extension or update: [https://unisat.io/download](https://unisat.io/download)

* * *

**Q: Can I convert my native $BABY token to BRC-20 BABY on Bitcoin via UniSat Wallet?** A: This feature is coming soon! Please stay tuned.

* * *

**Q: Why is the "My Staked BTC" amount displayed incorrectly on the UniSat wallet?** A: You can visit the [Babylon official website](https://babylonlabs.io/)
 to view the correct staked amount. This display issue will be fixed in the next version — thank you for your patience!

**Disclaimer:**

The content of this document is for informational purposes only. It does not constitute (i) investment advice or recommendations, (ii) an offer, solicitation, or inducement to purchase, sell, or hold digital assets, or (iii) financial, accounting, legal, or tax advice. Digital assets (including FTs and NFTs) are subject to market volatility, involve high risk, regulatory changes, and may depreciate in value. The information is provided "as is," and we make no warranties regarding its accuracy. Please consult your legal, tax, or investment professionals to determine whether trading or holding digital assets is suitable for you.

# Privacy Policy | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/legal/privacy-policy.md)
.

Last Updated: August 07, 2025

UniPro Technology Limited (“Company”, “we”, “our”, or “us”) recognizes that people who use our product value their privacy. This privacy policy (“Policy”) describes how the Company collects, uses, shares, and stores personal information of users of our website, [https://unisat.io](https://unisat.io/)
 (together with the sub-domains, collectively the “Site”). This Policy applies to the Site, applications, products and services (collectively, “Services”) on or in which it is posted, linked, or referenced.

By using the Services, you accept the terms of this Policy and our Terms of Use, and consent to our collection, use, disclosure, and retention of your information as described in this Policy. If you have not done so already, please also review our Terms of Use. The Terms of Use contain provisions that limit our liability to you and require you to resolve any dispute with us on an individual basis and not as part of any class or representative action. IF YOU DO NOT AGREE WITH ANY PART OF THIS POLICY OR OUR TERMS OF USE, THEN PLEASE DO NOT USE ANY OF THE SERVICES.

Please note that this Policy does not apply to information collected through third-party websites or services that you may access through the Services or that you submit to us through email, text message or other electronic message or offline. If you visit any links to third-party websites, pages, contents or platforms which may be accessible through links from the Site or any link of any of our third-party partners, you must and will be required to agree to and comply with the separate and independent privacy policy of such third parties. We will not bear any liability for the contents and activities of such sites operated by third parties.

If you are visiting this site from the European Union (EU), see our Notice to EU Data Subjects below for our legal basis for processing and transfer of your data.

**1\. WHAT WE COLLECT**

When you interact with the Services, information we collect from you includes:

*   Network information regarding transactions, only the publicly-availiable blockchain data, such as publicly-available blockchain address;
    
*   Contact information, such as your email address;
    
*   Feedback and correspondence, such as information you provide in your responses to surveys, when you participate in market research activities, report a problem with the Services, receive customer support or otherwise correspond with us;
    
*   Usage information, such as information about how you use the Services and interact with us;
    
*   Marketing information, such as your basic marketing preferences for receiving marketing communications. We will only collect data related to behavioral analytics, including your interactions with emails and advertisements (e.g., opens, clicks), if you have given your explicit consent.
    
*   Information We Get From Others. We may get information about you from other third party sources and we may add this to information we get from your use of the Services. Such information may include:
    
    *   Information Automatically Collected. We may automatically record certain information about how you use our Site (we refer to this information as “Log Data“). Log Data may include information such as a user’s Internet Protocol (IP) address, device and browser type, operating system, the pages or features of our Site to which a user browsed and the time spent on those pages or features, the frequency with which the Site is used by a user, search terms, the links on our Site that a user clicked on or used, and other statistics. We use this information to administer the Services and we analyze (and may engage third parties to analyze) this information to improve and enhance the Services by expanding its features and functionality and tailoring it to our users’ needs and preferences.
        
    
*   We use HTTP request headers to transmit session information, and we use local storage to support certain functionalities within the Site. For more information, please see the sections entitled “Local Storage Policy” below. We will update this policy and notify users if there are any changes.
    
*   We also use Google Analytics to help us offer you an optimized user experience. You can find more information about Google Analytics’ use of your personal data here: [https://policies.google.com/privacy?hl=en](https://policies.google.com/privacy?hl=en)
    .
    
*   Information we will never collect: (1)We will never ask you to share your private keys or wallet seed. Never trust anyone or any site that asks you to enter your private keys or wallet seed; (2) Our wallet app supports biometric login using your device’s built-in face or fingerprint authentication. All biometric data is processed locally by the device’s secure system (e.g., Apple Face ID, Android Biometric API). We do not collect, store, or transmit any biometric information.
    

**2\. USE OF PERSONAL INFORMATION**

To provide our Services, we use your personal information only where permitted by applicable law and for the limited purposes described below:

*   To enable you to access and use the Services
    
*   To provide and deliver products and services that you may request
    
*   To send information, including confirmations, technical notices, updates, security alerts, and support and administrative messages
    
*   To comply with law
    

We use your personal information as we believe necessary or appropriate to comply with applicable laws, lawful requests and legal process, such as to respond to subpoenas or requests from government authorities.

*   To communicate with you
    

We will not use your personal data for direct marketing activities unless we have obtained your prior written consent. With your consent, we may send you direct marketing communications that may be of interest to you, including but not limited to providing promotional information, upcoming events, and other news about products and services offered by us and our selected partners.

Personal data supplied by you will not be provided to third parties for direct marketing activities of products or services by such third parties unless we have obtained your prior written consent and such data transfer shall be in accordance with the requirements set out under the Personal Data (Privacy) Ordinance (Chapter 486 of the Hong Kong laws) and any other applicable law before we use your personal data for direct marketing activities.

If you do not wish to receive our direct marketing activities, you may opt out anytime by emailing us at contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
.

*   To optimize our platform
    

In order to optimize your user experience, we may use your personal information to operate, maintain, and improve our Services. We may also use your information to respond to your comments and questions regarding the Services, and to provide you and other users with general customer service.

*   With your consent
    

We may use or share your personal information with your consent, such as when you instruct us to take a specific action with respect to your personal information, or you opt into third party marketing communications.

*   For compliance, fraud prevention, and safety
    

We may use your personal information to protect, investigate, and deter against fraudulent, unauthorized, or illegal activity. These purposes are based on our legitimate interest in ensuring the security and integrity of our Services.

**3\. SHARING OF PERSONAL INFORMATION**

We do not share or sell the personal information that you provide us with other organizations without your express consent, except as described in this Policy or required by law or legal reporting obligations. We disclose personal information to third parties under the following circumstances:

*   Affiliates. We may disclose your personal information to our subsidiaries and corporate affiliates for purposes consistent with this Policy.
    
*   Business Transfers. We may share personal information when we do a business deal, or negotiate a business deal, involving the sale or transfer of all or a part of our business or assets. These deals can include any merger, financing, acquisition, or bankruptcy transaction or proceeding. If such a transfer occurs, we will require the recipient to continue to use your personal information in a manner consistent with this Policy or otherwise obtain your consent.
    
*   Compliance with Laws and Law Enforcement; Protection and Safety. We may share personal information for legal, protection, and safety purposes.
    
*   We may share personal information to comply with laws.
    
*   We may share personal information to respond to lawful requests and legal processes.
    
*   We may share personal information to protect the rights and property of the Company, our agents, customers, and others. This includes enforcing our agreements, policies, and Terms of Use.
    
*   We may share personal information in an emergency. This includes protecting the safety of our employees and agents, our customers, or any person.
    
*   Professional Advisors and Service Providers. We may share personal information with those who need it to do work for us. These recipients may include third party companies and individuals to administer and provide the Services on our behalf (such as customer support, hosting, email delivery and database management services), as well as lawyers, bankers, auditors, and insurers. All service providers with whom we share personal information are contractually obligated to protect the data in compliance with applicable privacy laws and use it only for the specific purpose of providing Services to us.
    
*   Other. You may permit us to share your personal information with other companies or entities of your choosing. Those uses will be subject to the privacy policies of the recipient entity or entities. We may also share aggregated and/or anonymized data with others for their own uses.
    

**4\. INTERNATIONAL TRANSFER**

We operate globally, and your personal data may be stored and processed in any country where we conduct business or where we engage service providers. We may disclose your information to third-party storage providers or affiliates located within your country/region, or transfer it to those located outside of your country/region.

We may also transfer your personal data to recipients in countries other than the one in which the data was originally collected. These countries may have different data protection or privacy laws from those in your own jurisdiction. However, we will take appropriate steps to ensure that any such transfers comply with applicable data protection laws and that your personal data remains protected to the standards described in this Policy. By using our Services, you acknowledge and agree to your personal information being processed in this way.

EU users should read the important information provided below about transfer of personal information outside of the European Economic Area (EEA).

**5\. HOW INFORMATION IS SECURED**

We retain information we collect as long as it is necessary and relevant to fulfill the purposes outlined in this Policy. In addition, we retain personal information to comply with applicable law where required, prevent fraud, resolve disputes, troubleshoot problems, assist with any investigation, enforce our Terms of Use, and other actions permitted by law. To determine the appropriate retention period for personal information, we consider the amount, nature, and sensitivity of the personal information, the potential risk of harm from unauthorized use or disclosure of your personal information, the purposes for which we process your personal information and whether we can achieve those purposes through other means, and the applicable legal requirements. If you stop using the Services or close your account on the Site, we will continue to retain your personal information as necessary to comply with our legal and regulatory obligations.

In some circumstances we may anonymize your personal information (so that it can no longer be associated with you) in which case we may use this information indefinitely without further notice to you.

We employ industry standard security measures designed to protect the security of all information submitted through the Services, including—where appropriate—data encryption (in transit and at rest), access controls, multi-factor authentication, regular security assessments, and secure coding practices. However, the security of information transmitted through the Internet can never be guaranteed. We are not responsible for any interception or interruption of any communications through the Internet or for changes to or losses of data. Users of the Services are responsible for maintaining the security of any password, biometrics, user ID or other form of authentication involved in obtaining access to password protected or secure areas of any of our digital services. In order to protect you and your data, we may suspend your use of any of the Services, without notice, pending an investigation, if any breach of security is suspected.

**6\. INFORMATION CHOICES AND CHANGES**

*   Accessing, Updating, Correcting, and Deleting your Information
    

You may access information that you have voluntarily provided through your account on the Services, and to review, correct, or delete it by sending a request to contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
. You can request to change contact choices, opt-out of our sharing with others, and update your personal information and preferences. We may need to verify your identity before processing such requests. In certain cases, we may retain information where required by law or for legitimate business purposes.

You may also opt out of receiving promotional communications from us by following the unsubscribe instructions in those messages. Please note that you may still receive transactional or service-related messages.

*   Google Analytics
    

You may exercise choices regarding the use of cookies from Google Analytics by going to [https://tools.google.com/dlpage/gaoptout](https://tools.google.com/dlpage/gaoptout)
 and downloading the Google Analytics Opt-out Browser Add-on.

*   CONTACT INFORMATION. We welcome your comments or questions about this Policy, and you may contact us at: contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
    .
    
*   CHANGES TO THIS POLICY. We may change this Policy at any time. We encourage you to periodically review this page for the latest information on our privacy practices. If we make any changes, we will change the Last Updated date above.
    

Any modifications to this Policy will be effective upon our posting of the new terms and/or upon implementation of the changes to the Site (or as otherwise indicated at the time of posting). In all cases, your continued use of the the Site or Services after the posting of any modified Policy indicates your acceptance of the terms of the modified Policy, except where your explicit consent is required under applicable laws.

**7\. ELIGIBILITY**

If you are under the age of majority in your jurisdiction of residence (“Minor Users”), you may use the Services only with the consent of or under the supervision of your parent or legal guardian. We do not knowingly offer Services to or collect personal information from Minor Users without verified parental/guardian consent. If we learn that we have received any information directly from Minor Users without first receiving his or her parent's verified consent, we will use that information only to respond directly to that Minor Users (or his or her parent or legal guardian) to inform the child that he or she cannot use the Site. We will then promptly delete that information from our records.

**8\. NOTICE TO EU DATA SUBJECTS**

*   Personal Information
    

With respect to EU data subjects, “personal information,” as used in this Policy, is equivalent to “personal data” as defined in the European Union General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR).

*   Sensitive Data
    

Some of the information you provide us may constitute sensitive data as defined in the GDPR (also referred to as special categories of personal data), including identification of your race or ethnicity on government-issued identification documents. We do not intentionally collect sensitive data, and where such data is collected, it is processed only where legally permitted and with appropriate safeguards.

*   Legal Basis for Processing
    

We only use your personal information as permitted by law. We are required to inform you of the legal basis of our processing of your personal information, which are described in the table below. If you have questions about the legal basis under which we process your personal information, contact us at contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
.

**Processing Purpose**

**Legal Basis**

To provide and deliver the Services

Contract performance (Art. 6(1)(b))

To send transactional or service communications

Legitimate interests (Art. 6(1)(f))

To send marketing communications

Consent (Art. 6(1)(a))

To optimize and maintain our platform

Legitimate interests (Art. 6(1)(f))

To ensure compliance, prevent fraud, ensure safety

Legitimate interests / Legal obligation (Art. 6(1)(c)/(f))

To respond to legal or data subject requests

Legal obligation (Art. 6(1)(c))

These processing activities constitute our legitimate interests. We make sure we consider and balance any potential impacts on you (both positive and negative) and your rights before we process your personal information for our legitimate interests. We do not use your personal information for activities where our interests are overridden by any adverse impact on you (unless we have your consent or are otherwise required or permitted by law).

*   With your consent
    

Where our use of your personal information is based upon your consent, you have the right to withdraw it anytime in the manner indicated in the Services or by contacting us at contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
.

*   Use for New Purposes
    

We may use your personal information for reasons not described in this Policy, where we are permitted by law to do so and where the reason is compatible with the purpose for which we collected it. If we need to use your personal information for an unrelated purpose, we will notify you and explain the applicable legal basis for that use. If we have relied upon your consent for a particular use of your personal information, we will seek your consent for any unrelated purpose.

*   Your Rights
    

Under the GDPR, you have certain rights regarding your personal information. You may ask us to take the following actions in relation to your personal information that we hold:

*   Opt-out. Stop sending you direct marketing communications which you have previously consented to receive. We may continue to send you Service-related and other non-marketing communications.
    
*   Access. Provide you with information about our processing of your personal information and give you access to your personal information.
    
*   Correct. Update or correct inaccuracies in your personal information.
    
*   Delete. Delete your personal information.
    
*   Transfer. Transfer a machine-readable copy of your personal information to you or a third party of your choice.
    
*   Restrict. Restrict the processing of your personal information.
    
*   Object. Object to our reliance on our legitimate interests as the basis of our processing of your personal information that impacts your rights.
    

You can submit these requests to contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
. We may request specific information from you to help us confirm your identity and process your request. Applicable law may require or permit us to decline your request. If we decline your request, we will tell you why, subject to legal restrictions. If you would like to submit a complaint about our use of your personal information or response to your requests regarding your personal information, you may contact us at contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
 or submit a complaint to the data protection regulator in your jurisdiction.

*   Cross-Border Data Transfer
    

Please be aware that your personal data will be transferred to, processed, and stored in jurisdictions outside your country of residence. Some data may be routed through or temporarily stored on servers located in the other countries for technical and operational reasons. Data protection laws in these jurisdictions may differ from those in your country of residence. You consent to the transfer of your information, including personal information, as described in this Policy by visiting our Site or using our Services.

Whenever we transfer your personal information out of the EEA to the countries not deemed by the European Commission to provide an adequate level of personal information protection, the transfer will be based on a data transfer mechanism recognized by the European Commission as providing adequate protection for personal information.

Please contact us if you want further information on the specific mechanism used by us when transferring your personal information out of the EEA.

**9\. LOCAL STORAGE POLICY**

To enhance your experience, we use browser-based local storage to temporarily save certain preferences and settings on your device. This data is stored locally and is not transmitted to our servers. Specifically, we store the following types of information in your browser:

*   Language Preference – To remember your selected language for future visits.
    
*   Wallet Connection Status – To recall the type of wallet you previously connected for quicker access.
    
*   Watchlist Items – To store digital assets or projects you follow, making them easier to revisit.
    
*   Local Identifier – To distinguish between devices or browsers, used solely for local interface optimization.
    

This local storage data is never used for advertising, user profiling, or tracking, and is accessible only within your own browser. It is not shared with third parties.

You may clear this data at any time via your browser settings. Please note that deleting local storage may affect certain features or preferences on the Site.

You represent and warrant that neither you, the legal entity you represent, nor any person(s) owning or controlling you or such legal entity, is located in, established under the laws of, or resident of any jurisdiction identified as a restricted region in our Restricted Jurisdictions Policy (the “Policy”). 1\. Technical and Infrastructure Terms

1.1 UniSat API Data Query Service: By accessing the UniSat API platform or using the API data query service provided by UniSat (the “API Service” or “Service”), including within a software application, website, tool, service, or product you create or offer to customers (your “Application”), you are agreeing to this Disclaimer and any other terms and conditions in respect of the API Service entered into by you and UniSat.

1.4 Service Changes and Termination: UniSat reserves the right to modify or terminate the API Service at any time at its sole discretion without prior notice. UniSat is not responsible for any losses or inconveniences resulting from such actions.

1.5 Data Accuracy: UniSat does not guarantee that the information returned by the API is accurate, timely, complete, valid, or error-free. UniSat is not responsible for any losses caused by inaccuracies, delay or data errors resulting from using the API Service.

1.6 Technical Compatibility: UniSat is not responsible for the incompatibility of the API with any devices, platforms, browsers, services, software or hardware. Users must ensure that their technical environment is compatible with the API Service.

1.7 Third-Party Content and Links: The API Service may contain content or links from third parties, solely as a convenience. Such third-party content or links are not under the control of UniSat and UniSat is not responsible for the quality, accuracy, completeness, performance or reliability of this content, nor for any losses incurred by Users using third-party content or accessing third-party links through the API. The fact that such content or links may be included in, provided by, or accessed through the API Service does not imply endorsement by UniSat or the third-party of either party’s sites or services. Depending on Users’ jurisdiction, some third-party content may not be available to Users.

1.9 Emergency Maintenance: UniSat has the right to perform emergency maintenance on the API Service at any time at its sole discretion, which may result in temporary service interruptions or suspensions. UniSat is not responsible for any losses or inconveniences caused by this. Nothing herein shall be construed as an obligation for UniSat to maintain or provide technical support of any kind for the API Service.

The fees and expenses of the arbitral tribunal shall be determined on the basis of \[Schedule 2/ Schedule 3\] of these Rules. The arbitration shall take place in Hong Kong, unless both you and us mutually agree on an alternative location;

(d) Class Action Waiver. YOU AND WE AGREE THAT EACH MAY BRING CLAIMS AGAINST THE OTHER ONLY ON AN INDIVIDUAL BASIS, AND NOT AS A PLAINTIFF OR CLASS MEMBER IN ANY PURPORTED CLASS OR REPRESENTATIVE PROCEEDING. YOU AND WE EXPRESSLY WAIVE ANY RIGHT TO FILE A CLASS ACTION OR SEEK RELIEF ON A CLASS BASIS. Unless both you and we agree, no arbitrator or judge may consolidate more than one person’s claims or otherwise preside over any form of a representative or class proceeding. The arbitrator may award injunctive relief only in favor of the individual party seeking relief and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by that party’s individual claim. If a court decides that applicable law precludes enforcement of any of this paragraph’s limitations as to a particular claim for relief, then that claim (and only that claim) must be severed from the arbitration and may be brought in court. If any court or arbitrator determines that the class action waiver set forth in this paragraph is void or unenforceable for any reason or that an arbitration can proceed on a class basis, then the arbitration provision set forth above shall be deemed null and void in its entirety and the parties shall be deemed to have not agreed to arbitrate disputes.

# InSwap Terms Of Use | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/legal/inswap-terms-of-use.md)

Last Updated: October 09, 2025

These InSwap Terms of Use (“Agreement”) constitute a binding agreement between you (“User” or “you”) and UniPro Technology Limited (“UniPro”, “we”, “us”, or “our”) and any of our affiliates regarding your access to and/or use of the InSwap services (collectively, “Services”). This Agreement applies to all actions you perform through InSwap.

By using our Services, you acknowledge that you have read, understood, and accepted this Agreement, the UniSat Terms of Use (“Service Terms”), the Privacy Policy, and any and all other rules or policies. Regardless of your jurisdiction, nationality, or the Services you use, you are legally bound by all terms and conditions herein. If you do not agree to be bound by this Agreement, do not access or use the Services. This Agreement supplements the Service Terms. In the event of any conflict or inconsistency between the terms of this Agreement and the Service Terms, such conflict or inconsistency shall be resolved by prioritizing this Agreement. All provisions of the Service Terms not modified by this Agreement shall remain in full force and effect. Any capitalized terms not defined herein shall have the meanings assigned to them in the Service Terms.

**1\. The Services.**

1.1 Product Description of InSwap.

InSwap is the first open-source AMM DEX for Bitcoin-Native Assets, built natively on Bitcoin and Fractal. The platform provides a seamless trading experience while maintaining the robust decentralization and security standards of Bitcoin assets, enabling Users to swap, provide Liquidity, and earn native protocol assets across both networks without intermediaries. For more detailed product information, please refer to [https://docs.unisat.io/services/products/inswap](https://docs.unisat.io/services/products/inswap)
.

1.2 Scope of Eligible Assets.

The Services currently support only Bitcoin-Native Assets. The following categories of assets are explicitly excluded:

1.  Legal and Regulatory Requirements: Assets deemed by resolution of a competent government authority, court ruling, or internal legal review to constitute regulated financial instruments (e.g., securities or derivatives);
    
2.  Fraud Allegations: Assets whose owners or controllers are subject to fraud allegations by major financial or criminal regulatory authorities;
    
3.  Intellectual Property Claims: Assets alleged by third parties to infringe intellectual property rights; and
    
4.  User Risks: Assets we determine to pose technical, fraudulent, or other risks that may materially compromise user safety.
    

1.3 Service Offerings.

Based on the characteristics of the AMM model, InSwap provides the following functions for Users to use:

1.  Create Trading Pair and Deploy Liquidity Pool;
    
2.  Deposit and withdraw funds from modules;
    
3.  Add or remove Liquidity;
    
4.  Send assets to other addresses within the module, assets are restricted to use within the module only; and
    
5.  Swap assets.
    

**2\. User Eligibility and Responsibilities.**

2.1 To use the Services, you must declare that you are of legal age, have the legal capacity to enter into contracts, and possess full legal authority to agree to and comply with this Agreement on behalf of yourself and any company/entity. You will not be accessing the Services in a jurisdiction where such Services are not permitted, restricted or illegal.

2.2 You further represent and warrant to us that you, the legal entity you represent, or any person(s) owning or controlling you or such legal entity, are not listed on any trade or economic sanctions lists maintained by any competent authorities, and you shall not engage in transactions with any listed parties, including but not limited to (1) the Islamic State of Iraq and the Levant (ISIL/Daesh), Al-Qaeda, the Taliban, and associated individuals, groups, enterprises, and entities; any individuals or entities designated by the United Nations Security Council or the United Arab Emirates; (2) the Democratic People’s Republic of Korea (DPRK), the Islamic Republic of Iran, or individuals, groups, enterprises, entities listed on sanctions lists maintained by the UN Security Council, the U.S. government (e.g., OFAC Sanctions List), the European Union or its member states, the Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS), the Hong Kong Monetary Authority (HKMA), the Hong Kong Customs and Excise Department, or other applicable governmental authorities; and (3) you are not a “U.S. Person” as defined under 17 CFR § 230.902. We reserve the right, at our sole discretion, to construe, limit, and modify the scope of Services in any jurisdiction at any time and to determine, at our sole discretion, your eligibility to access or use this Agreement and the Services. You acknowledge that such determinations may affect your ability to access or use the Services and agree to bear all associated risks and consequences. If you fail to meet such eligibility requirements, we may, temporarily or permanently, suspend, restrict or terminate your access to the Services.

2.3 You are solely responsible for managing your private keys and safeguarding your assets. We do not custody, possess, or control your assets. We assume no liability or obligation for your use of wallets. We shall not be liable for any claims, damages, losses, or liabilities arising from infringement of your wallet or assets.

2.4 Prohibited Activities. You affirm that your access to and use of the Services will fully comply with applicable laws and regulations, and you shall not engage in, promote, or facilitate any illegal activities through the Services, including but not limited to, money laundering, market manipulation, cyberattacks, fraud, or misrepresentation.

**3\. Fees and Payments.**

3.1 Network Fees. While accessing and using the Services, you are required to pay transaction fees to the relevant network.

3.2 Swap Fees. Every swap incurs a 1.5% transaction fee. 1/6 of this fee goes to the platform, while the rest is distributed among Liquidity Providers (LPs).

3.3 Third-Party Service Fees. During your access and use of the Services, additional third-party protocol fees may apply. You are solely responsible for paying any and all third-party protocol fees that may arise.

3.4 We reserve the right to modify fees at any time with or without prior notice to you.

**4\. Risks and Disclaimers.**

4.1 Market Volatility Risk. You acknowledge that asset prices are subject to uncontrollable factors such as market supply/demand, industry events, regulatory changes, and macroeconomic conditions, which may cause extreme price fluctuations or even total devaluation. You alone bear all losses arising from market volatility, including asset depreciation, slippage, or unexpected exchange ratios in Liquidity pools.

4.2 Fake Asset Risk. You understand that anyone may create assets, including counterfeit versions of existing assets or fraudulent claims of project representation. You accept the risk of inadvertently swaping such assets, and we assume no liability for losses caused by your participation in counterfeit asset transactions.

4.3 AMM Impermanent Loss Risk. Impermanent Loss refers to the potential loss where the value of Liquidity Provider (LP) assets falls below the value of holding the original assets due to price divergence within a Liquidity pool. This is an inherent feature of decentralized exchanges (DEX) directly tied to market volatility. By providing Liquidity, you fully acknowledge and voluntarily assume this risk, and we disclaim all liabilities for such losses.

4.4 Regulatory Risk. You recognize that digital asset markets are nascent, and regulatory policies vary across jurisdictions and may evolve, potentially adversely impacting service availability or asset value. You must ensure your use complies with local laws and regulations.

4.5 Technical Risk. We assume no liability for losses caused by technical failures, including hacker attacks, viruses, system adjustments, website upgrades, legal/government-mandated shutdowns, or third-party blockchain disruptions affecting InSwap’s operations.

4.6 Derivative Function Risks. You acknowledge that during your use of the Services, you assume risks arising from operating various features, including but not limited to: Burning LP, which will cause permanent lockup of Liquidity, forfeiture of assets, and irrevocable loss of all associated rights, value/utility.

4.7 Force Majeure. We disclaim responsibility for losses arising from force majeure events such as natural disasters, wars, network failures, or power outages or damages arising from any event beyond our reasonable control.

4.8 Non-Investment Advice. For the avoidance of doubt, we do not provide any financial, investment or legal advice in connection with the Services. Any incentives provided to enhance user experience, including those involving third-party community assets accessed through activities, shall not be construed as investment advice. Your use of the Services and participation in activities are unsolicited, self-directed, and solely your responsibility.

4.9 You agree that the above factors may lead to abnormal trading conditions, price fluctuations, market disruptions, or other irregularities. This disclaimer is non-exhaustive, and we reserve the right to perform special handling operations on certain assets or their transaction orders. You further agree that we assume no liability for losses related to these risks.

**8\. Dispute Resolution and Class Action Waivers.**

8.1 Any Dispute shall be first resolved through good faith negotiations. You must inform us of the Dispute by sending an email to contact legal @unisat.io. If no settlement is reached within sixty (60) days of our first email to commence the negotiations, the Dispute shall be resolved exclusively by arbitration in accordance with Section 8.2 below.

8.2 Any Dispute shall be referred to and finally resolved by arbitration administered by the Hong Kong International Arbitration Centre (HKIAC) in accordance with the HKIAC Administered Arbitration Rules in force when the Notice of Arbitration is submitted. The arbitrator may award injunctive relief only in favor of the individual party seeking relief and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by that party’s individual claim. If a court decides that applicable law precludes enforcement of any of this paragraph’s limitations as to a particular claim for relief, then that claim (and only that claim) must be severed from the arbitration and may be brought in court. If any court or arbitrator determines that the class action waiver set forth in this paragraph is void or unenforceable for any reason or that an arbitration can proceed on a class basis, then the arbitration provision set forth above shall be deemed null and void in its entirety and the parties shall be deemed to have not agreed to arbitrate disputes.

9.1 When using the Services, if there is any conflict between the terms of this Agreement and the Service Terms regarding the interpretation, division of responsibilities, or rights and obligations related to InSwap services, the explicit provisions of this Agreement shall prevail.

9.2 To this Agreement. We reserve the right, at our sole discretion, to add, modify or replace any part of this Agreement (including any Policies) at any time, and all modifications will be effective when they are posted. It is your responsibility to check this Agreement periodically for changes. Your continued use of or access to the Services following the posting of any changes to this Agreement constitutes acceptance of those changes. If you do not agree with any modifications to this Agreement, you must immediately stop accessing and using all of our Services.

9.3 If you encounter intellectual property infringement, need to report a complaint, or have a potential Dispute with us while using our Services, you may contact us by emailing contact [legal@unisat.io](mailto:legal@unisat.io)
 or through other official communication channels.

**10\. Definitions.**

“Bitcoin-Native Assets” means Cryptographic assets created directly on the Bitcoin blockchain or its core extension protocols, without relying on external independent blockchains, including but not limited to Ordinals, Alkanes, and related protocol assets to be created in the future.

“Automated Market Maker” or “AMM” means a decentralized exchange mechanism that facilitates peer-to-peer asset trading through algorithmically managed Liquidity pools, eliminating the need for traditional order books. Prices are determined by predefined mathematical formulas (e.g., constant product formulas), and Liquidity Providers contribute assets to these pools in exchange for proportional transaction fees and Liquidity pool assets.

“Liquidity” refers to digital assets deposited by users into algorithmically managed pools on InSwap to enable peer-to-peer trading through its Automated Market Maker (AMM) infrastructure. Liquidity is deployed in paired configurations and the ratio of assets within a pool determines exchange rates for swaps.

“LP/LPs (Liquidity Provider/Liquidity Providers)” means a user who contributes digital assets to a Liquidity pool on InSwap.

“slippage” means the difference between the price you expect to receive after swapping and what you actually receive after the swap is complete, occurring due to market volatility or insufficient Liquidity in a pool during the execution interval.

# UniSat Credits | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/unisat-credits.md)
.

What is Credits?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/unisat-credits#id-5b65)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Credit is a ‘virtual’ FB that can be used towards service fee offset and other benefits across our products. It is designed to reward users who have been long-term supporters and have made significant contributions to our network.

Please note that **Credit is not an on-chain asset. It is non-transferable, non-tradable, and can only be used within the UniSat ecosystem.**

The first batch of eligible addresses that will receive the Credits includes miners that have successfully mined blocks during Fractal Testnet, early testers of brc20 swap on Bitcoin mainnet, and users who had difficulties with claim during the Fractal Bitcoin Mainnet Bootstrap Program.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FKE1lGQFz3B5P5mDHR1qc%252F1.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dbb45f5df-97aa-4782-b952-a3f3af944f04&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=3b0257d495b5289d41dac27c4dd9b3e8&sv=3)

* * *

How Does Credits Work?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/unisat-credits#id-6221)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Credits can be used to offset Rollup Fee on InSwap and to offset service fees in UniSat Inscribe & Marketplace, replacing the FB or BTC you would typically use. When trading on InSwap or inscribing, simply select “Credits” for rollup fee deductions.

Over time, we plan to expand the use of these Credits to other UniSat services, such as UniSat Inscribe, UniSat Marketplace, and other features in the future.

* * *

Eligibility for Credits[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/unisat-credits#id-6dfb)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Users eligible to receive **Goodwill Credit** include:

1.  **Initially eligible users who were later unable to claim during the Fractal Bitcoin Mainnet Bootstrap Program**
    

*   90FB worth of credit in total for each address, with 15FB credits distributed monthly over six consecutive months.
    

**2\. Miner addresses that participated in Fractal Testnet mining and successfully mined blocks:**

*   Credit will be distributed based on the total number of successful blocks mined. Each block is worth 0.5 FB. For addresses that mined fewer than 40 blocks (inclusive), a minimum threshold of 20 FB worth of credit will be granted. The maximum cap is 1500 FB per address. Addresses with 0 blocks mined will not be eligible for this benefit.
    
*   For addresses eligible to receive more than 20 FB worth of Credits, the Credits will be distributed monthly with a cap of 20 FB worth of Credits each month until the full amount is dispersed.
    
*   If you are a Fractal testnet miner who has mined blocks but has not received any credits, please contact us and create a ticket on Discord [http://discord.gg/unisat](http://discord.gg/unisat)
    

**3\. Addresses that participated in early testing of the brc20-swap on the Bitcoin Mainnet:**

*   20 FB worth of credit for each address, distributed monthly over six consecutive months.
    

**4\. Addresses with 1,000 or more UniSat Points:**

*   If you have 1000 or more UniSat Points, you’re eligible to receive a total of 60FB worth of credit, with 10 FB Credits distributed monthly over six consecutive months.
    

**Note:** Monthly Credits not claimed in the current month will expire when next month’s Credits become available.

* * *

How to Get Started with the Credit?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/unisat-credits#d3ea)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  If you qualify, please visit [https://unisat.io/address/credit](https://unisat.io/address/credit)
     or connect your wallet and click on “Credits” to claim it.
    

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FdLztanjPZHTuoUXgCAAj%252F33.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D06b8c896-4bcd-4c76-a8e0-4e510d1fb2ce&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=68f74d75f7662ae234c8bc0a5e2a9d38&sv=3)

2\. When inscribing or trading on InSwap /Marketplace, select “Credits” for rollup fee deductions.

![](https://docs.unisat.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3523236551-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FJ4NHAHIVnWQiEecvs1By%252Fuploads%252FaZe3vJY0g393rEqiqvku%252F666.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D1b5829fd-ff9d-4216-809b-9117b47c6a07&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9d7d8a0d5ff54e8e4ecfedcd8911b573&sv=3)

**Disclaimer:**

The content of this document is for informational purposes only. # UniSat Titles | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/unisat-titles.md)
.

UniSat Titles are your personal collection of digital medals that celebrate your journey in the UniSat community!

Each Title marks a moment when you helped Bitcoin grow and thrive. As you collect more, you'll unlock exciting new ways to earn benefits from UniSat and our partners, starting with exclusive allocations in UniSat Drops.

Claim your titles here by connecting your wallet to verify eligibility: [https://unisat.io/address/my\_title](https://unisat.io/address/my_title)

There is no fee involved in claiming Titles. Titles are awarded for free and exist off-chain, making them non-transferable and non-tradable digital achievements.

💡This list keeps track of all available Titles, and will continue to be updated as more Titles are released.

*   **Diamond Hand**
    
    *   WZRD (brc-20) Diamond Hand
        
        *   Acquired/minted and held of any amount of $wzrd for more than 90 days consecutive
            
        
    *   Bitcoin Wizard (NFT) Diamond Hand
        
        *   Acquired/minted and held a Bitcoin Wizard for more than 90 days consecutive
            
        
    *   UniSat OG Pass Diamond Hand
        
        *   Acquired/minted and held a UniSat OG Pass for more than 90 days consecutive
            
        
    *   Prime Access Pass Diamond Hand
        
        *   Acquired and held a UniSat Prime Access Pass for more than 90 consecutive days.
            
        
    *   Early Access Pass Diamond Hand
        
        *   Acquired and held a UniSat Early Access Pass for more than 90 consecutive days.
            
        
    *   UniSat Emblem Diamond Hand
        
        *   Acquired and held a UniSat Emblem for more than 90 consecutive days.
            
        
    
*   **Product Pioneer**
    
    *   UniSat Points Hodler (>500)
        
        *   Collected more than 500 UniSat points, on Fractal and Bitcoin network combined.
            
        
    *   Early Contributor to brc-20 Swap
        
        *   Addresses eligible for early access to brc-20 swap module on Bitcoin during testing and Alpha Launch
            
        
    *   Fractal Inaugural Voters
        
        *   Voted in the first Fractal Vote and first Fractal Eco vote(include all 7 eco projects vote)
            
        
    

*   **Community Builder**
    
    *   UniSat Community Champion
        
        *   Received any UniSat emblem for participating in community events and still hold the emblem in the wallet
            
        
    *   Bitcoin Tech Carnival 2025
        
        *   Physical attendees of the Bitcoin Tech Carnival event during Consensus 2025 who has submitted their address during registration.
            
        
    *   Fractal Christmas Carnival 2024 Stamp Collector's Club
        
        *   Collected all 12 stamps in Fractal Christmas Carnival 2024
            
        
    *   Fractal Community Champion
        
        *   Winners in Fractal Bitcoin Community Events
            
        
    *   UniSat Discord Contributor
        
        *   Community members who earned the Community Supporter role in UniSat’s official Discord.
            
        
    

📍 Titles will be continuously added and adjusted based on our ongoing plans and arrangements.

You can view the details of different titles at: [https://unisat.io/address/my\_title](https://unisat.io/address/my_title)

# Ticker Verification Badge | UniSat

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.unisat.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge.md)
.

### What is the Ticker Verification Badge?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#what-is-the-ticker-verification-badge)

The Ticker Verification Badge (✔) highlights Tickers that are widely recognized by the community as the primary or canonical version of a ticker.In open ecosystems like brc-20 and Runes, multiple tickers may share identical or very similar names. This badge is designed to help users:

*   Identify the most commonly recognized version of a ticker
    
*   Avoid impersonations or misleading duplicates
    

Badges may appear across:

*   Marketplace listings
    
*   Marketplace Rankings and trending lists
    
*   Search results (UniScan) - Coming soon
    
*   Ticker detail pages - Coming soon
    

* * *

### Why is this badge important?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#why-is-this-badge-important)

Because brc-20 and Runes are permissionless, anyone can deploy Tickers using:

*   Similar naming conventions
    
*   Slight variations intended to confuse users
    

This can lead to users unintentionally trading the wrong ticker. The ticker Recognition Badge helps reduce this risk by signaling the version of the Ticker that is most widely recognized and used.

* * *

> Important: This badge does not imply that the ticker was deployed by an official project team, nor that UniSat has verified any “official issuer.” Many tickers in the ecosystem are community-created and decentralized in origin.

> The Ticker Recognition Badge does not:
> 
> *   Guarantee price performance or liquidity
>     
> *   Eliminate market risks
>     
> *   Represent endorsement or partnership by UniSat
>     
> *   Confirm legal, financial, or project legitimacy
>     
> 
> Users should always conduct their own research (DYOR) before interacting with any ticker.

* * *

### How are badges issued?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#how-are-badges-issued)

All badges are manually reviewed by the UniSat team. Evaluation may include (but is not limited to):

*   Community recognition and consensus
    
*   Trading activity and liquidity across platforms
    
*   Historical usage and distribution
    
*   Presence of impersonation or duplicate tickers
    
*   Public information such as websites or social channels
    

Badge decisions are discretionary and may be updated over time.

* * *

### How to request a badge[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#how-to-request-a-badge)

If you believe a ticker should receive a Ticker Recognition Badge, you can submit a request.

#### **🔸 Submission form:** [https://forms.gle/LQWaFQ7xk5Bc1jAV7](https://forms.gle/LQWaFQ7xk5Bc1jAV7)
[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#submission-form-https-forms.gle-lqwafq7xk5bc1jav7)

#### Who can apply?[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#who-can-apply)

You may apply if you are:

*   A community contributor or representative
    
*   A developer or early participant
    
*   An active stakeholder in the ticker ecosystem
    

* * *

#### What to include[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#what-to-include)

Please provide:

*   Ticker name and ticker
    
*   Ticker ID / Rune ID
    
*   Relevant links (website, Twitter, Discord, etc.)
    
*   Context explaining:
    
    *   Why is this ticker the widely recognized version
        
    *   Whether there are impersonations or duplicates
        
    *   Any supporting data (e.g. trading activity, community presence)
        
    

* * *

#### Review process[](https://docs.unisat.io/misc/knowledge-base/ticker-verification-badge#review-process)

*   All submissions are manually reviewed
    
*   The team may consider market data and cross-platform activity
    
*   Additional information may be requested
    
*   Approved tickers will receive the badge
    

Please note:

*   Submission does not guarantee approval
    
*   Badge status may be updated or removed if conditions change
